# 13 Identification of Band Structures in $^{137}\mathrm{La}^*$

Zhu Shengjiang $^{1}$ , Li Mingliang $^{1}$ , Xiao Shudong $^{1}$ , Che Xinglai $^{1}$ , Yu Yingnan $^{1}$ , Ding Huaibo $^{1}$ , Zhu Lihua $^{2}$ , Li Guangsheng $^{2}$ , Wen Shuxian $^{2}$ , Wu Xiaoguang $^{2}$ , Wang Zhimin $^{2}$

(1. Department of Physics, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China;

2. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

The nucleus $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ lies in the $A = 135$ transitional region with the proton and neutron numbers approaching the closed shells at $Z = 50$ and $N = 82$ . In this region, the nuclei have a small $\beta_{2}$ value and a

soft $\gamma$ -deformation. The proton Fermi surface lies in the lower $h_{11/2}$ subshell, whereas the neutron Fermi surface lies in the upper $h_{11/2}$ subshell. Thus, the rotational alignment of a pair of $\mathbf{h}_{11/2}$ protons tends to drive the nucleus to a near prolate ( $\gamma \sim 0^\circ$ ) shape, while the rotational alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ neutrons tends to drive the nucleus to a near oblate ( $\gamma \sim -60^\circ$ ) shape [1]. Therefore, the different quasiparticle configurations can drive a nucleus to different shapes and sometimes shape coexistence may be observed in a nucleus. It is expected that at low spin the $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ nucleus should have stronger single-particle property than the $N < 80$ neighboring La isotopes as its neutron number is more close to the closed shell at $N = 82$ . In order to systematically understand the nuclear structure character, it is interesting to search for the collective band structure with higher spin states in $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ nucleus.

High spin states in $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ were populated through the reaction $^{130}\mathrm{Te}(^{11}\mathrm{B}, 4\mathrm{n})^{137}\mathrm{La}$ at a beam energy of $50\mathrm{MeV}$ . An isotopically enriched $^{130}\mathrm{Te}$ target of thickness $1.6\mathrm{mg/cm}$ was bombarded by the beam of $^{11}\mathrm{B}$ ions accelerated by the HI-13 tandem accelerator at the China Institute of Atomic Energy (CIAE), Beijing. The gamma-gamma coincidence measurement was carried out using an array of fourteen Compton-suppressed Ge detectors. Approximately $1.8\times 10^8$ coincidence events were collected. The level scheme of $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ obtained in the present work is shown in Fig. 1, which was based on the $\gamma-\gamma$ coincidence relationships, the relative transition intensities and the DCO ratio analysis. Some of the levels and transitions observed in Ref. [2] were confirmed in the present work. All levels and transitions above the $15/2$ level were newly identified. Four collective bands observed are labeled on the top of the scheme. A cluster of levels labeled (5) in Fig.1 is complex and probably has single-particle property.

The lowest levels in $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ are mainly of proton $g_{7/2}$ and $d_{5/2}$ single-quasiparticle nature[2]. The single-proton $h_{11/2}$ is probably the main component of the configuration of the $11/2^{-}$ state. The $13/2^{-}$ and $15/2^{-}$ states have been assigned to the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes 2^{+[2]}$ .

Band (1) based on the $17/2^{-}$ state is constructed definitely in our work with spin up to $29/2^{-}$ . There are strong M1 transitions observed inside the band. This band is probably originated from the $\pi d_{5/2} \otimes \mathrm{vd}_{3/2}^{-1} h_{11/2}^{-1}$ configuration. A band crossing (backbending) occurs at $h\omega - 0.48$ MeV. Based on the systematic comparison with the neighboring nuclei, we propose that the observed band crossing of band (1) in $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ probably originates from alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ protons.

Band (3) has strong $\Delta I = 1$ dipole transitions and no crossover transitions are observed. Based on a systematic comparison with the neighboring nuclei, this collective band should belong to a collective rotational oblate band built on a multiquasiparticle configuration with $-60^{\circ}$ . Some cranked shell model calculations have been carried out to understand the different shape driving effects of protons and neutrons in this region[1]. It shows that the alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ neutrons favors an oblate shape $(\gamma - -60^{\circ})$ . The oblate bands in this region exhibit some distinct properties: (a) much stronger $\Delta I = 1$ transitions relative to the $\Delta I = 2$ transitions inside the band, (b) no signature splitting occurring, and (c) different momenta of inertial from those of prolate bands. Band (3) observed in $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ shows such characteristics. As the band head energy of band (3) is much higher than that in band (1), band (3) may belong to a five-quasiparticle band. The possible configuration can be assigned as $\pi g_{7/2}d_{5/2}h_{11/2} \otimes [\nu h_{11/2}]^2$ .

Band (2) might be based on three-quasiparticle configuration. Band (4) should have multiquasi-

particle configuration. Assigning their configurations needs more works.

![](images/6c956bbb78a729c8e964b8995a24acb103db5ef3f661c3ab9e9df4aa17c62c8f.jpg)  
Fig.1 Level scheme of $^{137}\mathrm{La}$

# References

[1] Paul et al. Phys Rev Lett, 1987, 58: 984.   
[2] Kortelahti M, et al. Nucl Phys, 1982, A376, 1.

# 中文摘要

利用重离子核反应与在束 $\gamma$ 谱的实验技术，对 $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ 核的高自旋态进行了研究。通过 $\gamma -\gamma$ 符合与DCO分析，建立了 $^{137}\mathrm{La}$ 新的能级纲图，将自旋态扩展到 $33 / 2\hbar$ ，并识别了4个集体转动带结构。通过系统学比较以及理论分析，对于建立在 $17 / 2^{-}$ 态的晕带结构的组态及带交叉特性进行了讨论，认为此带可能属于3个准粒子带，其组态为 $\pi d_{5 / 2}\otimes \nu d_{3 / 2}^{-1}h_{11 / 2}^{-1}$ ，其带交叉可能是由一对质子的顺排所致。而另一个建立在 $15 / 2^{-}$ 能级上的带则属于具有5个准粒子组态的扁椭形变带，其组态可能为 $\pi g_{7 / 2}d_{5 / 2}h_{11 / 2}\otimes [\nu h_{11 / 2}]^2$ 。

# 14 High Spin States in $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}^*$

Zhu Shengjiang $^{1}$ , Yu Yingnan $^{1}$ , Ding Huaibo $^{1}$ , Xu Ruiqing $^{1}$ , Li Mingliang $^{1}$ , Xiao Shudong $^{1}$ , Che Xinglai $^{1}$ , Zhu Lihua $^{2}$ , Li Guangsheng $^{2}$ , Wen Shuxian $^{2}$ , Wu Xiaoguang $^{2}$ , Wang Zhimin $^{2}$

(1. Department of Physics, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China;

2. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

The $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ nucleus is located in the $N = 135$ neutron-deficient mass region. The proton Fermi surface lies in the bottom of the $h_{11/2}$ subshell, while the neutron Fermi surface lies in the top of $h_{11/2}$ subshell. Thus, the rotational alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ protons tends to drive the nucleus to a near prolate $(\gamma - 0^{\circ})$ shape, while the rotational alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ neutrons tends to drive the nucleus to a near oblate $(\gamma - -60^{\circ})$ shape[1]. Therefore, the different quasiparticle configurations can drive a nucleus to different shapes and sometimes shape coexistence may be observed in a nucleus. The experimental data of level scheme in $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ is not sufficient as comparing with the neighboring nuclei. In the this report, we present the new high spin states of $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ obtained by using the heavy ion reaction.

The high-spin states in $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ were populated with the heavy-ion fusion-evaporation reaction $^{124}\mathrm{Sn}(^{16}\mathrm{O}, 4\mathrm{n})^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ . The corresponding experiment was performed at Beijing Tandem Accelerator Nuclear Physics Laboratory in CIAE. The $^{16}\mathrm{O}$ ions with beam energies 73 MeV collide with the $^{124}\mathrm{Sn}$ target to induce the fusion-evaporation reactions. First, the fusion- evaporation excitation functions were measured and compared with the theoretical calculations. After the excitation function measurement, the $\gamma$ -rays emitted from the evaporation residues were measured with 14 HPGe detectors. The $\gamma$ - $\gamma$ coincidence events were stored in the computer. After subtracting the background, we collect $1.4\times 10^8 \gamma$ - $\gamma$ coincidence events. A coincidence matrix and a matrix of directional correlation from oriented nuclei (DCO) were established in the data pre-treatment. The format was converted as Radware on Linux platform.

The new level scheme of $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ is shown in Fig.1. Comparing our results with those in previous publications[2,3], the level scheme has been significantly updated and many new levels and transitions are observed. Band (1) is confirmed by the present work which has been reported in Refs. [2,3]. This band was proposed as a magnetic rotational band with four quasiparticle $\mathsf{v}h_{11/2}^{-2} \otimes \pi g_{7/2} h_{11/2}$ configuration[3] having a small oblate deformation ( $\varepsilon_2 \sim 0.11$ and a tilt angle of $32^\circ$ ). Bands (2) and (3) are newly observed in our work. Based on the systematical comparison with the neighboring nuclei, we believe that the band (2) belongs to an oblate band with $\gamma \sim 60^\circ$ . Some cranked shell model calculations have been carried out to understand the different shape driving effects of protons and neutrons in this region[1]. It shows that alignment of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ neutrons favors an oblate shape ( $\gamma \sim -60^\circ$ ). The oblate bands in this region exhibit some distinct properties: (a) much stronger $\Delta I = 1$ transitions relative

to the $\Delta I = 2$ transitions inside the band, (b) no signature splitting occurring, and (c) different momenta of inertial from those of prolate bands. Band (2) observed in $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ shows such characteristics. This oblate band may belong to a four-quasiparticle band. The possible configuration can be assigned as $\pi d_{5/2} g_{7/2} \otimes v h_{11/2}^{-2}$ .

![](images/c4ca85ee022d9b6bb1f25380ea6ccccd98c4cbc74d258e1b3a1857cf13dcbfec.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Level scheme of $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$

Band (3) has $\Delta I = 2$ transitions inside the band. This band has the high deformation characteristics. Fig. 2 and 3 show the kinetic momentum of inertia $(J_{1})$ and dynamical momentum of inertia $(J_{2})$ against the rotational frequency $\hbar \omega$ in band (3) of $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ as well as in the high deformation or the super deformation bands in the neighboring Ce isotopes reported in the previous publications. They have very similar character. From the $J_{2}$ value, one can deduce the deformation parameter of the band (3) in $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ with $\beta_{2} \sim 0.42$ . This band probably belongs to a four quasiparticle band. The proposed configuration is $\mathrm{vi}_{13/2}^2 \otimes \pi h_{11/2}^2$ . Thus, the alignments of a pair of $h_{11/2}$ protons and a pair of $i_{13/2}$ neutrons of intrude orbitals drive the nucleus to prolate shape with high deformation.

![](images/b5a89097bc699a634478f9707a07071c2ca49f9858f50d194fdb54cd3f5a6647.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Kinetic momentum of inertia $J_{1}$ against rotational frequency $\hbar \omega$

![](images/ef3b2a4f837e5ba238d5931200f7a8f5312e8eff97022958d19539105f53dc47.jpg)  
Fig. 3 Dynamical momentum of inertia $J_{2}$ against rotational frequency $\hbar \omega$

# 中文摘要

利用重离子核反应与在束 $\gamma$ 谱的实验技术，对 $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ 核的高自旋态进行了研究。通过 $\gamma -\gamma$ 符合与DCO分析，建立了 $^{136}\mathrm{Ce}$ 新的能级纲图，着重对高自旋作了扩展。分析表明，建立在 $14^{-}$ 态上的转动带验证了国外一研究组的结果，具有 $\varepsilon_{2}\sim 0.11$ 弱形变的磁转动带；另一个带为新发

现的基于 $12^{\pm}$ 态的正字称带，为具有 $\gamma - 60^{\circ}$ 的扁椭形变带，而基于 $10^{\pm}$ 态的新的正字称带，由 $\Delta I = 2$ 的E2跃迁构成，分析表明，此带为 $\beta_{2} \sim 0.42$ 的高形变带，由一对 $h_{11/2}$ 质子和一对 $i_{13/2}$ 中子的顺排同时驱动核向长椭形变，产生在较低自旋态下的高形变带。

# 15 The High Spin Structures of the Odd-Odd Nucleus $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}^*$

He Chuangye $^{1}$ , Zhu Lihua $^{1}$ , Wu Xiaoguang $^{1}$ , Wen Shuxian $^{1}$ , Li Guangsheng $^{1}$ , Wang Zhimin $^{1}$ , Cui Xingzhu $^{1,2}$ , Zhang Zhenlong $^{1,2}$ , Meng Rui $^{1,2}$ , Ma Ruigang $^{1}$ , Yang Chunxiang $^{1}$

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China;

2. Department of Physics, Jilin University, Changchun 130023, China)

Chirality is recently observed in high spin states of triaxial nuclei. It is a new symmetry introduced to nuclear rotation. It has been suggested that chiral twin bands might be observed in the $A\sim 130$ , $A\sim 100$ and $A\sim 180$ transitional region[1]. The first experimental evidence for chirality structures, based on a $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \mathrm{vh}_{11/2}$ configuration was reported recently in several $N=75$ , $N=73$ , $N=71$ isotones. Considerable effort has been made over the last two years to search for chirality structures in $A\sim 100$ mass region. Very recently, the experimental evidence for chirality was found in the $\pi g_{9/2} \otimes \mathrm{vh}_{11/2}$ configuration of ${}^{104}\mathrm{Rh}^{[2]}$ . ${}^{106}\mathrm{Ag}^{[3]}$ is the nearest $N=59$ isotones of ${}^{104}\mathrm{Rh}$ , and has great possibility to find chiral twin bands in this nucleus.

The high-spin states of $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}^{[3]}$ were populated via the fusion-evaporation reaction $^{100}\mathrm{Mo}(^{11}\mathrm{B}, 5\mathrm{n})^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ at a beam energy of $60\mathrm{MeV}$ . The $^{11}\mathrm{B}$ beam was delivered by the HI-13 tandem accelerator of the China Institute of Atomic Energy (CIAE). The target consisted of a $2.5\mathrm{mg/cm}^2$ layer of $^{100}\mathrm{Mo}$ enriched to $97.4\%$ and evaporated on an $11\mathrm{mg/cm}^2$ lead backing. A total of $130\times 10^6 \gamma-\gamma$ coincident events were collected in event-by-event mode. Fig. 1 shows the partial level scheme of $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}^{[3]}$ deduced from the present work.

# 1) The positive bands

In the left side of the level scheme in Fig.1, the $6^{+}$ state is a known isomer, a configuration of $\pi(g_{9/2})^{-3} \otimes \mathrm{v}d_{5/2}$ coupled with the $^{100}\mathrm{Sn}$ core. Below the $12^{+}$ state, there are 2 bands with strong E2 transitions, in which the right band has M1 transitions between each levels, whereas the left one has M1 transitions only from the state of even spin to the state of odd spin. In the former work[3], the right band is interpreted as a coupled band with configuration of $\pi g_{9/2} \otimes \mathrm{v}g_{7/2}$ , and the left one is a semi-decoupled band with configuration of $\pi g_{9/2} \otimes \mathrm{v}d_{5/2}$ . The levels above $12^{+}$ state are the members of the band built on the configuration of unpaired $h_{11/2}$ neutrons. This band has much stronger M1 transitions than the E2 transitions, being possible magnetic band built on the configuration of unpaired $h_{11/2}$ neutrons.

# 2) The negative bands

The band (2) in Fig.1 is a new band identified in our experiment, and its intensity is very weak. The E2 transitions are much weaker than the M1 transitions in this band. For the assignment of its

configuration, a further analysis and theoretical calculations are needed.

The negative band (3) is the yrast band of $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ . It has stronger M1 transitions and weaker E2 transitions, and is assigned as $\pi g_{9/2} \otimes v h_{11/2}$ [3] configuration. The yrast band (4) is similar as band (3), the energies of the levels between $13^{-}$ and $16^{-}$ are very close to that of band (3), especially for the $14^{-}$ state, the energy difference is only $39\mathrm{keV}$ . Comparing to the chiral bands observed in this mass region, we suggest that band (3) and band (4) are possible chiral twin bands of $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ .

The further analysis of above experimental data is under way.

Fig. 1 Partial level scheme of $^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$   
![](images/0921f5f950479e26404f1e7ee1cac519981398dd0ec7f1134758b7e284634589.jpg)  
Energies are in keV; $\gamma$ transitions indicated by \* are newly identified in present experiment

[2] Vaman C, Fossan D B, Starosta K, et al. Phys Rev Lett, 2004, 92: 032501.   
[3] Jerrestam D, Klamra W, Gizon J, et al. Nucl Phys, 1994, A577: 786.

# 中文摘要

利用能量为 $60\mathrm{MeV}$ 的 ${}^{11}\mathrm{B}$ 束流，通过 ${}^{100}\mathrm{Mo}(^{11}\mathrm{B},5\mathrm{n})^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ 熔合蒸发反应布居 ${}^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ 的高自旋态，用15台带BGO反康的HPGe探测器进行在束 $\gamma$ 谱学测量。通过 $\gamma -\gamma$ 符合矩阵开窗分析和DCO比值分析，建立了 ${}^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ 新的能级纲图，增加了26条新的 $\gamma$ 跃迁和10条新能级。通过与其相邻同位素奇奇核的系统比较，对 ${}^{106}\mathrm{Ag}$ 的4条转动带的组态及结构性质进行了初步讨论。

# 16 Structure of High Spin States in $^{52}\mathbf{Mn}^*$

Wu Xiaoguang<sup>1</sup>, Zhu Lihua<sup>1</sup>, Wen Shuxian<sup>1</sup>, Li Guangsheng<sup>1</sup>, Zhang Zhenlong<sup>1,2</sup>, Meng Rui<sup>1,2</sup>, Cui Xingzhu<sup>1,2</sup>, He Chuangye<sup>1</sup>, Wang Zhimin<sup>1</sup>, Ma Ruigang<sup>1</sup>, Yang Chunxiang<sup>1</sup>

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China;

2. College of Physics, Jilin University, Changchun 130023, China

The present experiment was carried out at HI-13 tandem accelerator of the China Institute of Atomic Energy in Beijing. High spin states in $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ , were populated via $^{12}\mathrm{C} + ^{48}\mathrm{Ti}$ fusion evaporation reactions at beam energy of 55-85 MeV. The target was a $^{48}\mathrm{Ti}$ foil with a thickness of $1.5\mathrm{mg/cm^2}$ , backed by a lead layer of about $20\mathrm{mg/cm^2}$ to stop the recoils. Gamma-gamma coincidence experiment was performed with an array consisting of fourteen Compton suppressed HPGe-BGO spectrometers. A total of $1.9\times 10^8$ coincidence events was accumulated on fixed disk in event by event mode. In the off-line analysis the event-by-event data were carefully gain-matched before they were stored into two dimensional $E_{\gamma} - E_{\gamma}$ matrices. The $\gamma$ -ray coincidence relationships were established by setting gates on the photo peaks of individual transitions and background subtractions performed by Radware, GASPware and specplot.

A new rotational band of $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ (Fig.1) has been established in this experiment, In order to get insight into the structure of the high spin states of $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ observed in present work, we have performed semi-empirical shell model (SESM) calculations, the results were agree with the experimental results (Fig.2).

Fig.1 Level scheme of $^{52}\mathbf{Mn}$   
The asterisk * indicate the new rotational band

![](images/23f6011b7408224d3616aafea0c8dcf5adbc0fc63671d27042487be8451e8ef8.jpg)  
Fig. 2 New level scheme of $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ proposed from this experiment, compared with the results of semi-empirical shell model (SESM) calculations

* Support by National Natural Science Foundation of China (10105015, 10175070), and Major State Basic Research Development Program (G2000077405)

# 中文摘要

通过重离子熔合蒸发反应 $^{12}\mathrm{C} + ^{48}\mathrm{Ti}$ 布局 $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ 核的高自旋态。在本次实验中使用14台高纯锗反康谱仪组成的 $\gamma$ 探测器阵列测量 $\gamma -\gamma$ 符合。通过对实验数据的分析，发现了 $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ 核的一条新的转动带，应用经验壳模型对 $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ 核进行了理论计算与分析，其结果与我们在实验中测量得到的数值相吻合。新的实验结果更进一步丰富了 $^{52}\mathrm{Mn}$ 核的能级纲图和核结构信息。

# 17 Lifetime Measurement of the High Spin States in $^{178}\mathrm{Os}^*$

Wu Xiaoguang<sup>1</sup>, Zhu Lihua<sup>1</sup>, Cui Xingzhu<sup>1,2</sup>, He Chuangye<sup>1</sup>, Liu Ying<sup>1</sup>, Wang Zhimin<sup>1</sup>, Li Lihua<sup>1</sup>, Li Guangsheng<sup>1</sup>, Wen Shuxian<sup>1</sup>, Yu Yingnan<sup>3</sup>, Chen Yongjing<sup>3</sup>, Wang Shuo<sup>4</sup>

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China;

2. College of Physics, Jilin University, Changchun 130023, China;   
3. Department of Physics, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China;   
4. Department of Physics, Peking University, Beijing 100871, China)

The present experiment was carried out at HI-13 tandem accelerator of the China Institute of Atomic Energy in Beijing. High spin states in $^{178}\mathrm{Os}$ were populated via ${}^{29}\mathrm{Si} + {}^{154}\mathrm{Sm}$ fusion evaporation reaction at beam energy of 155 MeV. The target was a $^{154}\mathrm{Sm}$ foil with a thickness of $1.4\mathrm{mg/cm^2}$ , backed by a lead layer of about $20\mathrm{mg/cm^2}$ to stop the recoils. Gamma-gamma coincidence experiment was performed with an array consisting of fourteen Compton suppressed HPGe-BGO spectrometers. A total of $1.23\times 10^8$ $\gamma$ - $\gamma$ coincidence events were accumulated on disk in event-by-event mode. In the off-line analysis the event-by-event data were carefully gain-matched before they were

sorted into two dimensional $E_{\gamma}E_{\gamma}$ matrix. The $\gamma$ -ray coincidence relationships were established by setting gates on the photo peaks of individual transitions and background subtractions using Radware package. Lifetimes of high spin states in $^{178}\mathrm{Os}$ are determined by analyzing the Doppler-broadened line shapes.

The Doppler-broadened lineshapes were analyzed for the forward angle spectra and backward angle spectra, and were fitted using DSAMFT code. As an example, Fig.1 shows the observed and fitted lineshapes for the $432\mathrm{keV}8^{+}\rightarrow 6^{+}$ transition at forward and backward angles. The extracted lifetimes of the g transitions between the states of $8^{+}$ and $16^{+}$ are listed in table 1.

Channel number   
Fig.1 The measured (open squares) and DSAMFT fitted (solid lines) line-shapes of $432\mathrm{keV}\gamma$ -rays at forward angle (a) and backward angle (b)

# 中文摘要

通过重离子熔合蒸发反应 ${}^{29}\mathrm{Si} + {}^{154}\mathrm{Sm}$ 布局 ${}^{178}\mathrm{Os}$ 核的高自旋态，束流能量为 $155\mathrm{MeV}$ 。使用14台高纯锗反康谱仪组成的 $\gamma$ 探测器阵列测量 $\gamma -\gamma$ 符合，通过开窗分析，分别得到前角和后角的 $\gamma$ 谱，利用多谱勒位移衰减法（DSAM）对前角和后角的 $\gamma$ 谱多谱勒展宽峰形状进行分析，提取能级寿命。分别测量到的 ${}^{178}\mathrm{Os}$ 核 $8^{+}$ 态到 $16^{+}$ 态之间的能级寿命。

# 18 Signature Inversion in Doubly-Odd Nucleus $^{158}$ Tm

Ma Yingjun<sup>1</sup>, Zhou Wenping<sup>1</sup>, Zhao Yanxin<sup>1</sup>, Li Xianfeng<sup>1</sup>, Zhao Guangyi<sup>1</sup>, Lu Jingbin<sup>1</sup>, Li Mingfei<sup>1</sup>, Wang Shouyu<sup>1</sup>, Sun Liang<sup>1</sup>, Yang Dong<sup>1</sup>,

Liu Yunzuo<sup>1</sup>, Zhu Lihua<sup>2</sup>, Wu Xiaoguang<sup>2</sup>, Cui Xingzhu<sup>2</sup>, He Chuangye<sup>2</sup>

(1. Department of Physics, Jilin University, Changchun 130023, China;

2. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

The doubly-odd nucleus $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ , with $Z = 69$ and $N = 89$ , lies at the edge of the well deformed region above the $N = 82$ magic number. It exhibits softness with respect to the quadrupole deformation parameter $\beta$ and triaxiality parameter $\gamma$ due to the shallow minima of the nuclear potential energy surface. In connection with this characteristic feature, remarkable phenomena such as band termination at high spin and signature inversion at low spins have been observed in previous studies[1-3] of this nucleus. Based on the systematic study[3] of signature inversion associated with the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes v i_{13/2}$ configuration in doubly-odd nuclei of the $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ region, an inversion spin of spin $I \approx 21\hbar$ at which the anomalous signature splitting at low spins is restored to normal signature splitting at high spins is expected. The main purpose of the present work is thus to try to find the expected inversion spin, which was not revealed in previous experimental studies of $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ , through in-beam measurements.

The experiment was performed at CIAE. The $^{19}\mathrm{F}$ beams with energies of 108 and 112 MeV delivered from the HI-13 tandem accelerator were bombarded on a $5\mathrm{mg/cm}^2$ self-supporting target enriched in $^{144}\mathrm{Nd}$ . A large number exit channels were open, resulting in the population of excited states in $^{157,158,159}\mathrm{Tm}$ , $^{157,158,159}\mathrm{Er}$ and $^{155}\mathrm{Ho}$ etc, among which the $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ nucleus was produced with the largest yields at both beam energies. A detector array consisting of 14 Compton-suppressed HPGe detectors was employed to record the $\gamma$ rays exited from the evaporation residues. These detectors were calibrated with standard $^{152}\mathrm{Eu}$ source for determining the energies and relative intensities of the recorded $\gamma$ rays. About 200 million $\gamma-\gamma$ coincidence events were accumulated. These events were sorted into a symmetric $4096\times 4096$ two-dimensional $E_{\gamma}E_{\gamma}$ matrix for the analysis of $\gamma-\gamma$ coincidence relationships. In order to obtain information on multipolarities of $\gamma$ rays, a non-symmetric so-called ADO (Angular distribution ratios of $\gamma$ -rays from oriented nuclei) matrix has also been constructed using conventional method.

The level scheme of $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ obtained in the present work is shown in Fig. 1, where the levels have been arranged in the bands labeled from B1 to B8. Assignment of the level scheme is based on their relative intensities, energy sums and coincidence relationships. Spin-parity assignments to the levels are based on the following considerations: 1) The previous knowledge[1-3] about $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ ; 2) Probable multipolarities inferred from the measured ADO ratios; 3) Presumption that the level spin increases regularly with increasing excitation energy.

B1 and B2 form a strongly coupled band, and this band has been known[1-3] as being built on the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes v i_{13/2}$ configuration at low spins. In comparison with the results obtained in previous studies[1,2], a new transition with energy of $882\mathrm{keV}$ has been added onto the top of B1, and a $814\mathrm{keV}\gamma$ ray has been assigned as the $24^{-} \rightarrow 22^{-}$ transition in B2. The strongly coupled band composed of B3 and B4,

![](images/678f5b7c370066483a6d54aca342e4fc7f86d526097f0651084c8ab75fa8face.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Level scheme of $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ as deduced the present work

identified in Ref. [1,2], is confirmed in the present work. In addition, a $17^{+}$ state has been identified in this work. This strongly coupled band was assigned to the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu h_{9/2} \otimes (\nu i_{13/2})^2$ configuration in previous studies[1,2]. The same conclusion is drawn from this study. Experimental evidence for band termination at spin $I = 38$ in B3 was reported in Ref. [1], and this observation is also confirmed in our work. B5 and B6 form a strongly coupled band which had been reported in Ref. [2]. However, it was tentatively connected to lower known states through a 738 keV transition. In the present work, the previous observation of 738 keV transition is confirmed, and a new 774 keV linking transition has been established. On the basis of several considerations as discussed in detail in Ref. [4], B5 and B6 are assigned to the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu h_{9/2} (\alpha = -1/2) \otimes (\nu i_{13/2})^2$ configuration. Accordingly, B3 and B4 are assigned to the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu h_{9/2} (\alpha = +1/2) \otimes (\nu i_{13/2})^2$ configuration[4]. The strongly coupled band composed of B7 and B8 is identified from this work. It is probably a 2-quasiparticle band built on the

$\pi g_{7 / 2}\otimes \mathsf{vi}_{13 / 2}$ configuration.

In Fig. 2 and Fig. 3, we show the plots displaying the signature splitting in energy as a function of spin for some coupled bands in $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ . For comparison, similar plots of several adjacent doubly-odd nuclei are also shown. From Fig.2, it is seen that the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \mathrm{vi}_{13/2}$ band in $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ shows no restoration of the low-spin inverted signature splitting to normal signature splitting at the spin $I \approx 21\hbar$ expected from systematics[3]. Instead, it shows persistent anomalous signature splitting up to the highest observed spin. Very similar behavior is also observed in the neighboring isotonic doubly-odd nucleus $^{160}\mathrm{Lu}$ . From Fig.3, it is seen that, while the 4-quasiparticle band based on the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \mathrm{vf}_{7/2} \otimes (\mathrm{vi}_{13/2})^2$ configuration in $^{162}\mathrm{Lu}$ and $^{162}\mathrm{Tm}$ shows normal behavior of signature splitting in energy, the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \mathrm{vh}_{9/2} \otimes (\mathrm{vi}_{13/2})^2$ bands from the $N = 89$ doubly-odd isotones $^{156}\mathrm{Ho}$ , $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ and $^{160}\mathrm{Lu}$ all show inverted signature splitting. These novel observations as displayed in Fig.2 and Fig.3 would present new physics underlying the phenomenon of signature inversion in rotating nuclei.

![](images/435d523b5873cde7c22c2af9b100a9e0940c414d949713f35d9acef9c2d9a20e.jpg)

![](images/1dec41e4526f5c1d09f86f30d7c6a34691a48928f8bbea4642c98cebb5ad57ed.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Plots of $S(I)$ - $I$ for the $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \mathsf{vi}_{13/2}$ bands in $^{158}\mathbf{Tm}$ and $^{160}\mathbf{Lu}$ where $S(I) = E(I \to I - 1) - [E(I + 1 \to I) + E(I - 1 \to I - 2)] / 2$

![](images/5c1477134284564314c040370966759a95a005918eebab364277dc83b17c1e6b.jpg)  
Fig. 3 $S(I)$ - $I$ plots for bands in some doubly-odd nuclei around $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$

# 中文摘要

通过融合蒸发反应 ${}^{144}\mathrm{Nd}({}^{19}\mathrm{F},5\mathrm{n})$ 布居了双奇核 $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ 的高自旋态，扩展了原来已知的带结构，并建立了一条新转动带。通过与相邻核的比较，讨论 $^{158}\mathrm{Tm}$ 核中两条四准粒子带的内禀组态，并分别指定为 $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu h_{9/2} (\alpha = +1/2) \otimes (\nu i_{13/2})^2$ 和 $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu h_{9/2} (\alpha = -1/2) \otimes (\nu i_{13/2})^2$ 组态。建立在 $\pi h_{11/2} \otimes \nu i_{13/2}$ 组态上的转动带被观测到呈现持续旋称反转现象，而前述的两条高自旋区的四准粒子转动带也呈旋称反转。这两种新型的旋称反转现象为旋称反转问题注入了新的研究内容。

# 19 High-Spin States in $^{152}\mathbf{Gd}$

Hua Hui $^{1}$ , Wang Shuo $^{1}$ , Meng Jie $^{1}$ , Li Zhihuan $^{1}$ , Zhang Shuangquan $^{1}$ , Xu Furong $^{1}$ , Liu Hongliang $^{1}$ , Ye Yanlin $^{1}$ , Jiang Dongxin $^{1}$ , Zheng Tao $^{1}$ , Wang Quanjin $^{1}$ , Chen Zhiqiang $^{1}$ , Wu Cuie $^{1}$ , Zhang Gaolong $^{1}$ , Pang Danyang $^{1}$ , Wang Jia $^{1}$ , Lou Jianlin $^{1}$ , Guo Bin $^{1}$ , Jin Ge $^{1}$ , Zhou Shangui $^{2}$ , Zhu Lihua $^{3}$ , Wu Xiaoguang $^{3}$ , Li Guangshen $^{3}$ , Wen Shuxian $^{3}$ , He Chuangye $^{3}$ , Cui Xingzhu $^{3}$ , Liu Ying $^{3}$

(1. School of Physics and MOE Key Laboratory of Heavy Ion Physics, Peking University, Beijing 100871, China;

2. Institute of Theoretical Physics, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Beijing 100080, China;

3. China Institute of Atomic Energy, Beijing 102413, China)

High-spin states of $^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ have been studied via the $^{148}\mathrm{Nd}(^{9}\mathrm{Be}, 5\mathrm{n})^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ fusion-evaporation reaction at beam energy 54 MeV. The ground-state band, octupole band and Beta band have been extended up to spins $20^{+}$ , $21^{-}$ and $16^{+}$ , respectively. A possible aligned two quasi-neutron band, which becomes the yrast band above the spin $16^{+}$ , was observed for the first time. The kinematic behaviors of the ground-state bands and yrast bands in $^{152,154}\mathrm{Gd}$ indicate that the shape of $^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ may change with the increasing of rotational frequency and the difference of quadrupole deformations between $^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ and $^{154}\mathrm{Gd}$ is obviously reduced around the first band crossing region, which are consistent with the theoretical calculations using Cranked Woods-Saxon-Strutinsky methods. The first band crossing observed in $^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ can be ascribed to the alignment of a pair of $i_{13/2}$ neutrons. The identical ground-state bands in $^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ and $^{154}\mathrm{Dy}$ were extended.

# 中文摘要

对于质量数在150左右的稀土区核，随着中子数的增加，原子核形状经历一个从球形到长

椭形的迅速变化。对这些形状过渡区核性质的研究为核理论模型提供了一个很好的检验手段。利用中国原子能院串列加速器提供的 $54\mathrm{MeV}$ 的 ${}^{9}\mathrm{Be}$ 束流，通过 ${}^{148}\mathrm{Nd}(^{9}\mathrm{Be}, 5\mathrm{n})$ 重离子熔合蒸发反应，对核素 ${}^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ 的高自旋态进行了研究。在 ${}^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ 核中发现了 10 多个新的高自旋能级和一个新的 S 带结构；实验中首次观察到 ${}^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ 核完整的第一个带交叉。通过对 ${}^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ 核运动学转动惯量的研究，发现了 ${}^{152}\mathrm{Gd}$ 核形状随着自旋变化的线索。

# 20 Measurement of $g$ -Factors for Magnetic Rotational Band in $^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$

Yuan Daqing, Zheng Yongnan, Zhou Dongmei, Zuo Yi, Du Enpeng,

Duan Xiao, Wang Chaohui, Wu Xiaoguang, Zhu Lihua,

Li Guangsheng, Xu Guoji, Zhu Shengyun

(China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-50, Beijing 102413, China)

Recent interest has been focused on magnetic rotation in nuclear structure studies. Magnetic rotation is a new mode of nuclear rotation, which is different from the conventional nuclear rotation based on deformed mass distribution of the nucleus. The magnetic rotation occurs in nearly spherical nuclei. At the head state of magnetic band a perpendicular coupling of the proton and neutron angular momenta is energetically favored. Along with the increasing of the excitation energy or spin angular momentum the angular momentum vectors of the neutron and proton move towards each other like the blades of a pair of shears. The nuclear $g$ -factor is a direct and sensitive probe of the nuclear angular momentum alignment and coupling. Therefore, the present work is motivated to measure the $g$ -factors of high spin states for the magnetic rotational band in ${}^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ in order to examine the proton and neutron alignment and coupling as a function of spin or excitation energy, to understand the mechanism of magnetic rotation, to provide experimental evidence for the existence of magnetic rotation and to check theoretical models.

The high spin magnetic states in $^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ are populated by the fusion-evaporation reaction $^{60}\mathrm{Ni}(^{28}\mathrm{Si}, 3\mathrm{p})^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ with a 98 MeV Si beam from the HI-13 tandem accelerator at China Institute of Atomic Energy. Transient-magnetic-field ion implantation perturbed angular distribution (TMF-IMPAD) method is used to determine the $g$ -factors of the magnetic band with a band head of $17/2^{-}$ . A three layer target of $^{60}\mathrm{Ni}-\mathrm{Fe}-\mathrm{Cu}$ is employed in the experiment: a $0.44\mathrm{mg}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$ target layer of $^{60}\mathrm{Ni}$ enriched to $99.6\%$ is evaporated onto an well annealed natural Fe layer of $1.56\mathrm{mg}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$ . A Cu stopper layer of $14\mathrm{mg}\cdot\mathrm{cm}^{-2}$ is evaporated on the other side of the Fe layer. After evaporation the three layer target is annealed again to make the Cu stopper layer crystallized and defect-free. The ferromagnetic layer is polarized by a $0.17\mathrm{T}$ magnetic field produced with an electromagnet. The direction of the polarizing magnetic field is perpendicular to the beam-detector plane and automatically reversed every $120\mathrm{s}$ during the measurement. The emitted $\gamma$ -rays are detected by four HPGe detectors at $\pm 60^{\circ}$ and $\pm 120^{\circ}$ to the beam direction. The $\gamma$ -coincidence data are recorded in multi-parameter event-by-event mode with the Kodaq data acquisition system. A total of $5\times 10^{8}$ coincidence counts are obtained in $220\mathrm{h}$ beam time. The magnetic rotational band is observed and the $\gamma$ -ray peak of the band head state contains $4.8\times 10^{4}$ counts. The data analysis of $g$ -factors for the states in the magnetic rotational band is still under way.

# 中文摘要

在中国原子能院的HI-13串列加速器上利用 ${}^{60}\mathrm{Ni}({}^{28}\mathrm{Si}, 3\mathrm{p})^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ 熔合蒸发反应布局 ${}^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ 的高自旋态磁转动带， ${}^{28}\mathrm{Si}$ 束流能量是 $98\mathrm{MeV}$ 。采用瞬态场-离子注入扰动角分布(TMF-IMPAD)方法测量带头为 $17/2^{-}$ 磁转动带态的 $\mathbf{g}$ 因子。实验靶是 ${}^{60}\mathrm{Ni}-\mathrm{Fe}-\mathrm{Cu}$ 三层靶，Fe的极化磁场强度为0.17T，其方向每 $120\mathrm{s}$ 自动翻转1次。4个与束流方向成 $\pm 60^{\circ}$ 和 $\pm 120^{\circ}$ 的HPGe探测器记录发射的 $\gamma$ 射线，用Kodaq数据获取系统以事件方式获取 $\gamma-\gamma$ 符合计数。

$^{85}\mathrm{Zr}$ 的高自旋态磁转动带 $g$ 因子的数据处理和分析正在进行中。

# 21 Precise Measurement of $^7$ Be Decay Rate in Au

Li Chengbo, Meng Qiuying, Liu Zhiyi, Fu Yuanyong, Wen Qungang, Zhou Jing, Zhou Shuhua (China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

Electron-capture decay rates depend sensitively on the density of atomic electrons near the nucleus, thus, physically and chemically environmental factor such as pressure, chemical form, magnetic fields etc., which can change electron densities, may affect electron-capture decay rates. Since the change of nuclear decay in different environments has fundamental significance, as well as application in nuclear physics, astrophysics, geology and condensed matter physics, such a study is of current interests. Furthermore, $^7\mathrm{Be}$ is the lightest radioactive nucleus that decays by electron-capture with a half-life of about 53 days, thereby, it is a good candidate for studying perturbation of nuclear decay rates. Changes of decay rate of $^7\mathrm{Be}$ nuclei in different host materials in which $^7\mathrm{Be}$ nucleus is located have been measured at normal pressure. The variations in the electron-capture decay rates are caused by the changes of the electron density near the nuclei, which is considered to depend on the lattice structure and the electron affinity of the host materials. Since Au is stable in chemical character, its electron affinity is easy to measure, and the data are consistent, thus, the precise decay rate of $^7\mathrm{Be}$ in Au host material at normal pressure and normal temperature is an important basic value to be compared with.

In the experiment, the $^7\mathrm{Be}$ source in Au host is prepared by irradiating the natural Au foil with $^7\mathrm{Be}$ beams from the radioactive secondary beam line Giraffe of the HI-13 tandem accelerator at China Institute of Atomic Energy. The $^7\mathrm{Be}$ beam energies are 21.6 MeV and 32.3 MeV, which are created by 34 MeV $^7\mathrm{Li}$ bombarded onto the hydrogen gas target in the primary target hall.

The crystal lattice structure of Au foil can be destroyed in the preparation process, in order to repair the damage of the crystal lattice structure, the Au foil is put in the vacuum of about $0.003\mathrm{Pa}$ , and heat it up to its anneal temperature for one hour, then take it out after it cools down naturally to the room temperature in the vacuum. The Au foil is annealed again to remove the damage of lattice after irradiation by $^{7}\mathrm{Be}$ beam.

By electron capture, the nucleus $^{7}\mathrm{Be}$ decays to the ground state of $^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ directly with a branching ratio of $89.5\%$ and to the first excited state with a ratio of $10.5\%$ , which decays subsequently to its ground state by emitting $478~\mathrm{keV}$ gamma rays. In this experiment, a high purity germanium (HPGe) detector is used to measure the $478~\mathrm{keV}$ gamma ray photons.

The sample of $^7\mathrm{Be}$ source in Au is cooled for 133 days after irradiation to reduce the influence of short-lived isotopes produced during the irradiation as much as possible. In order to decrease the background caused by the environment, the sample is put in the shield which is made of (from inside to outside) the organic glass, aluminum, copper, and lead.

The measurement is conducted for about 260 days, that is about 5 times of the half-life of $^7$ Be decay. Fig.1 is a typical gamma ray spectrum of $^7$ Be decay in Au, and the Fig.2 is the curve of decay rate via time. The decay constant and half-life of $^7$ Be in Au can be extracted then by fitting the curve with an exponential function.

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lambda (\mathrm {A u}) = (1. 3 0 1 8 6 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2} \pm 8 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7}) \mathrm {d} ^ {- 1} \\ T _ {1 / 2} (\mathrm {A u}) = (5 3. 2 4 3 \pm 0. 0 0 3) \mathrm {d} \\ \end{array}
$$

The half-life of $^{7}\mathrm{Be}$ decay in $^{197}\mathrm{Au}$ measured before is $(53.311 \pm 0.042) \, \mathrm{d}$ given by $\mathrm{Norman}^{[1]}$ , and it is $(53.270 \pm 0.019) \, \mathrm{d}$ given by LIU Zhiyi[2]. But, the samples have not been annealed before measurement in these two experiments. The two results are a little higher than our experiment value, but the differences are less than $0.1\%$ considering their errors.

![](images/68a462a133cce686fddd20698e21f7bc1118ead5bdc48a294a1cc15ca92fe284.jpg)  
Fig. 1 A typical $\gamma$ -spectrum of $^7$ Be decay in $^{197}$ Au

![](images/096b7b84e2bb187c1f043d491fe71844082ba0aaf44c1782035b00f52b68cf2b.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Curve of decay rate via time of $^7\mathrm{Be}$ in $^{197}\mathrm{Au}$

# References

[1] Norman E B, Rech G A, Browne E, et al. Influence of physical and chemical environments on the decay rates of $^{7}$ Be and $^{40}$ K [J]. Phys Lett, 2001, B519: 15.   
[2] Liu Z Y, Li C B, Wang S G, et al. High precision measurement of half-life of $^7$ Be [J]. Nucl Phys Rev, 2003, 20: 235.

# 中文摘要

放射性核素的半衰期特别是发生电子俘获和内转换过程的半衰期与核外电子密度有关，因此，改变核外物理、化学环境就有可能改变核素的半衰期。精确测量这些核素的半衰期以及研究它们在不同核外环境中半衰期的变化在许多领域有着重要的应用。从环境物质电子亲合势对电子俘获过程的影响这一角度看，Au的化学性质稳定，电子亲合势的测量数据比较一致，注入到Au中的 $^{7}\mathrm{Be}$ 的衰变率适于作为参照。因此，在常温和常压下的 $^{7}\mathrm{Be}$ 在Au中衰变率的精确值是非常重要的物理量。

Au样品中 $^7\mathrm{Be}$ 源的制备是在中国原子能科学研究院HI-13型串列加速器的放射性次级束流线上进行的。为避免制靶过程中机械碾压工艺及 $^7\mathrm{Be}$ 离子注入过程对靶材料晶格结构的影响，Au靶在 $^7\mathrm{Be}$ 注入前后都做了退火处理。照射后，正式测量前将样品冷却133d以降低辐照中产生的短半衰期的放射性本底。为减少环境本底，测量在屏蔽体中进行。测量持续进行了4.9个半衰期，充分考虑了死时间修正、本底扣除和误差处理等技术，最后得到了 $^7\mathrm{Be}$ 在 $^{197}\mathrm{Au}$ 中的衰变常数和半衰期数值分别为：

$$
\begin{array}{l} \lambda (\mathrm {A u}) = (1. 3 0 1 8 6 \times 1 0 ^ {- 2} \pm 8 \times 1 0 ^ {- 7}) \mathrm {d} ^ {- 1} \\ T _ {1 / 2} (\mathrm {A u}) = (5 3. 2 4 3 \pm 0. 0 0 3) \mathrm {d} \\ \end{array}
$$

此前，Norman[1]测量的 $^7\mathrm{Be}$ 在Au中衰变的半衰期为 $(53.311\pm 0.042)$ d，刘志毅[2]的结果为 $(53.270\pm 0.019)d$ 。在这两个实验中，测量前没有对样品进行退火处理。它们的值都略高于本实验结果。但考虑到给出的误差，两者与本测量结果的差别小于 $0.1\%$

# 22 Research on EUV Spectra of Highly Ionized Titanium

Yang Zhihu<sup>1</sup>, Du Shubin<sup>2</sup>, Zeng Xiantang<sup>2</sup>, Ren Shoutian<sup>2</sup>,

Song Zhangyong<sup>1</sup>, Su Hong<sup>1</sup>, Wang Youde<sup>1</sup>

(1. Institute of Modern Physics, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Lanzhou 730000, China;

2. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-46, Beijing 102413, China)

The foil-excited the spectrum of highly stripped titanium ions between $12 - 40\mathrm{nm}$ has been studied. Titanium ions of 80 and $120\mathrm{MeV}$ were provided by the HI-13 tandem accelerator at China Institute of Atomic Energy. GIM-957 XUV-VUV monochromator was refocused to obtain highly-resolved spectra. Our experimental results and the published spectral data of laser-produced plasma show agreement in nearly all cases within $\pm 0.03\mathrm{nm}$ . The spectra contained some of both week and stronger lines previously unclassified. These spectral lines mainly belong to $2s2p^2$ for Ti XVIII, $2p^3$

for Ti XVIII, $2\mathrm{s}2\mathrm{p}^3$ for Ti XVII, $2\mathrm{p}^6 4\mathrm{p}$ for Ti XII and $2\mathrm{p}^6 3\mathrm{d}$ for Ti XII transitional lines. Fig.1 shows a partial spectrum in the experiment at 80 and $120\mathrm{MeV}$ . A large part the identified transitions are given in table 1 together with previous observations and theoretical wavelengths.

# 23 Study of the Lifetime of Highly Ionized Atoms

Du Shubin<sup>1</sup>, Chang Hongwei<sup>1</sup>, Yang Zhihu<sup>2</sup>, Li Jingwen<sup>1</sup>, Jiang Liyang<sup>1</sup>, Yang Haozhi<sup>1</sup>, Huang Min<sup>3</sup>

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-46, Beijing 102413, China;

2. Institute of Modern Physics, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Lanzhou 730000, China;

3. Fudan University, Shanghai 200433, China)

The study of lifetime for highly ionized atoms is important for testing basic atomic theory. Beam-foil spectroscopy is an important part in this field. Using beam-foil technique, transition lines of highly ionized atoms have been widely observed. The beam-foil method has also been applied in lifetime determinations[1,2]. There are also several theoretical papers, e.g. the work by Ivanova et al.[3] and the semiempirical study by Curtis and Ramanujam[4] in the lifetime study for highly ionized atoms.

The experiment was carried out at HI-13 tandem accelerator in China Institute of Atom Energy (CIAE). The semi-empirical formula of Nikolaev and Dimitriev[5] was used to select desired atomic species and the beam energy. The beam, being collimated with an aperture of $3.5\mathrm{mm}$ in diameter, passed through a carbon foil with thickness of $25~\mu \mathrm{g} / \mathrm{cm}^2$ in target chamber, then was collected in a Faraday cup $20\mathrm{cm}$ away from the spectrometer entrance slit. In the experiments the ion currents is $0.2\mu \mathrm{A}$ . The energy loss in the foil was taken into account when calculating the final beam particle velocities. Photons emitted by the foil-excited ions were detected by a $2.2\mathrm{m}$ grazing-incidence scanning monochromator, equipped with 300 and 600 lines/mm gratings and a CCD detector at the exit slit. The spectrometer viewed the beam at an angle of $90^{\circ}$ . The spectrometer entrance slit was positioned very close to the ion beam ( $5\mathrm{mm}$ from the beam) to give a narrow window, which is a practical necessity for measuring short lifetimes. Doppler broadening of the lines due to the finite but small entrance aperture was negligible in this spectral region. The wavelength accuracy is generally $5.0\times 10^{-12}\mathrm{m}$ , taking into account the instrumental resolution and other uncertainties. The lifetime data were taken by moving the exciter foil, and stored directly in a computer. The short lifetime of ions studied in our experiments ( $0.01\mathrm{ns} < \tau < 0.1\mathrm{ns}$ ) could be measured because of the high velocity of the ion beam (about $10^{10}\mathrm{cm/s}$ ), which allowed observation of the radiative decay over a few millimeters. The movement of the carbon foil along the beam axis was limited to $10\mathrm{cm}$ , which was sufficient to detect cascades. Lifetimes were extracted from the decay data with program CANDY.

In present work we have analyzed the excited configurations of Si XIII 1s3d-1s4f, Si XII

$1\mathrm{s}^{2}3\mathrm{d}-1\mathrm{s}^{2}4\mathrm{f}$ , Fe XVI $2\mathrm{p}^{6}(\mathrm{^1S})$ 4p—2p ${}^{6}(\mathrm{^1S})$ 5d and ${}^{2}\mathrm{P}_{3/2}^{0}-{}^{2}\mathrm{D}_{3/2}$ as well as $\Delta n=0$ transitions of S ( $2\mathrm{s}^{2}$ ${}^{1}\mathrm{S}_{0}-2\mathrm{s}2\mathrm{p}{}^{1}\mathrm{P}_{1}^{0}, 2\mathrm{s}2\mathrm{p}{}^{1}\mathrm{P}_{1}^{0}-2\mathrm{p}^{2}\mathrm{S}_{0}, 2\mathrm{s}2\mathrm{p}{}^{3}\mathrm{P}_{0}^{0}-2\mathrm{p}^{2}\mathrm{P}_{1}$ ). The lifetime values are 40, 45, 79.9, 129, 81 and 165 ps. So far, beam-foil technique is the only way to get lifetime values in this range.

我们分析了Si XIII 1s3d—1s4f、Si XII 1s²3d—1s²4f、Fe XVI 2p⁶(¹S) 4p—2p⁶(¹S) 5d，光谱项为²P³/²—²D₃/₂，波长为12.470 nm、Δn=0的S跃迁的能级寿命参数。现有文献做的是波长较长的谱线，寿命在ns数量级，我们选择了短波长，寿命在10ps数量级。这对实验精度提出了很高的要求，也大大增加了实验和分析的难度。我们的结果为Si XIII结构为1s3d—1s4f光谱项是¹D₂—¹F₂°的跃迁，波长11.092 nm、能级寿命为40ps；Si XII 1s²3d—1s²4f²D₃/₂—²F₅/₂跃迁波长为12.953 nm、能级寿命为45ps。Fe XVI 2p⁶(¹S) 4p—2p⁶(¹S) 5d光谱项为²P³/₂—²D₃/₂，实验结果为79.9 ps，理论计算结果是83.33 ps。目前，尚未见到其他实验室的实验值。对S测量了2s²¹S₀—2s2p¹P₀₁、2s2p¹P₀₁—2p²¹S₀、2s2p³P₀—2p²³P₁等5个结构的数据，其中，2s2p³P₀—2p²³P₁首次给出实验数据。目前，在此寿命范围的实验测量只有束箔方法。

# 1 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ Measurement With Accelerator Mass Spectrometry at China Institute of Atomic Energy

He Ming $^{1}$ , Jiang Shan $^{2}$ , Peng Bo $^{1,2}$ , Ruan Xiangdong $^{2}$ , Dong Kejun $^{1}$ , Guan Yongjing $^{1}$ , Li Shihong $^{1}$ , Wu Shaoyong $^{1}$ , Qiu Jiuzi $^{1}$ , Yin Xinyi $^{1}$ , Ding Yanqiu $^{1,2}$

(1.China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-50, Beijing 102413, China;

2. Department of Physics, Guangxi University, Nanning 530004, China)

# 1 Introduction

${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ is an anthropogenic radionuclide produced in large quantities in nuclear weapon testing, nuclear fuel cycle and nuclear medicine. The very long half-life $(2.1\times 10^{5}\mathrm{a})$ of ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ and the high solubility and high potential for the pertechnetate anion $\left(\mathrm{TcO}_4^-\right)$ to migrate into the environment has led ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ to be widely distributed in environment. The release of ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ might constitute a public health problem, but meanwhile provide an artificial tracer for the study of the movement of this nuclide in river and ocean, in the food chain and in biological system. A sensitive detection technique of ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ is necessary and important. It is very difficult to measure ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ at low level by decay counting methods. A number of mass spectrometric methods have been developed to measure ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ such as thermal ionization mass spectrometry (TIMS)[1], inductively-coupled plasma mass spectrometry(ICP-MS)[2], and resonance ionization mass spectrometry[3]. Recently, AMS has also been developed to measure ${}^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ [4-6].

The measurement method of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ with AMS has been developed at the CIAE. Sample preparation procedures for the separation of technetium and ruthenium have also been developed. $\mathrm{Nb}_2\mathrm{O}_5$ was used as the sample matrix and for AMS system normalization. In the AMS measurement, $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ and $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ were identified differently in the final detector system.

# 2 Sample Preparation

In AMS measurements of long-lived radionuclides the stable isotopes of the radioisotopes of interest are used as chemical carriers, sample matrices and internal yield monitors and for instrument normalization. But for $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ , there is no stable isotope. Accordingly, suitable methods were developed in the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ sample preparation to circumvent this problem.

In order to monitor the chemical yield and to check the capability of chemical separation of $\mathrm{Tc}$ from Ru, the radioisotopes $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\mathrm{m}}$ and $^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ were produced and used as the tracers. The $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\mathrm{m}}$ was synthesized using the $^{95}\mathrm{Mo}(\mathrm{p},\mathrm{n})$ reaction. A natural Mo foil was irradiated with 9 MeV protons provided by the CIAE HI-13 accelerator. The produced $\mathrm{Tc}$ is chemically separated from the Mo foil by dissolution and subsequent column separation using an anion exchange resin as described in reference [7]. The $^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ is produced using the $^{102}\mathrm{Ru}(\mathrm{n},\gamma)^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ reaction. The $\mathrm{RuCl}_3$ was irritated by thermal neutrons from the heavy water reactor at the CIAE, and then dissolved with de-ionized water.

There are several methods available for the separation of Ru from $\mathrm{Tc}^{[8,9]}$ . Here we used technetium-specific resin (Eichrom TEVA-spec $^{\text{TM}}$ ) to separate these two elements. The radioisotopes $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\text{m}}$ and $^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ were used as the tracers to check the separation. Typically $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\text{m}}$ and $^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ of 10 Bq was mixed together.

The solution was then rinsed through about $3\mathrm{mL}200$ mesh TEVA ion exchange resin. The column was then washed with $10\mathrm{mL}$ $\mathrm{H}_2\mathrm{O}$ and $10\mathrm{mL}1\mathrm{mol} / \mathrm{L}$ $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ to elute ruthenium. Finally the technetium was eluted with $10\mathrm{mL}8\mathrm{mol} / \mathrm{L}$ $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ . The separation of technetium and ruthenium and the chemical recovery were determined via $\gamma$ -ray spectrometry of $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\mathrm{m}}$ and $^{103}\mathrm{Ru}$ . The results showed that the decontamination coefficient for Ru was at least 2000, while the chemical recovery of the technetium was about $98\%$ . For the environmental samples, $10\mathrm{Bq}^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\mathrm{m}}$ was added to one liter aqueous samples and $5\mathrm{mL}$ peroxide was added to oxidized technetium for $\mathrm{TcO_4}^-$ , the technetium eluate was obtained by passing the resulting solution through the TEVA resin as described above.

Niobium was selected as a sample matrix. The Nb powder was dissolved with concentrated HF and $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ at $150~^\circ \mathrm{C}$ , and converted to an $\mathrm{HNbO_4}$ solution by adding $\mathrm{NH_4OH}$ and $\mathrm{H}_2\mathrm{O}_2$ . For each sample, about $2\mathrm{mg}$ Nb in the form of $\mathrm{HNbO_4}$ solution was added to the technetium eluate, and evaporated for drying at about $70^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ . Once dried, the niobium oxide pellet was transferred to a porcelain crucible and baked for one hour at $450~^\circ \mathrm{C}$ . The sample was then crushed, mixed with high purity aluminum powder, and pressed into an aluminum AMS sample holder. The results from the $^{95}\mathrm{Tc}^{\mathrm{m}}$ tracer indicate typical overall recovery of about $95\%$ for the sample preparation.

# 3 AMS Measurement

AMS measurement was performed using the CIAE AMS system. Negative ions were extracted from the cesium sputter source (MC-SNICS), $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}^{16}\mathrm{O}^{-}$ ions were selected by the injection magnet and injected into the accelerator, and $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}^{10+}$ ions of 90 MeV were selected by the high-energy spectrometer. Before this, $^{93}\mathrm{Nb}^{16}\mathrm{O}^{-}$ was extracted and injected into the accelerator. The $^{93}\mathrm{Nb}^{10+}$ was used to simulate the transmission of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}^{10+}$ in the AMS system. We selected the accelerator terminal voltage of 8.25 MV for the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ measurement because of the limitation of an electrostatic analyzer in our AMS system. Then the beam current of $^{93}\mathrm{Nb}^{10+}$ was measured with a Faraday cup located after the analyzing magnet, and the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}^{10+}$ was detected with a four-anode gas ionization detector filled with P10 gas of 85 mbar. Differential energy loss was measured with the anode signals of $\Delta E_{1}, \Delta E_{2}, \Delta E_{3}$ and $E_{\mathrm{r}}$ . Total energy $(E_{\mathrm{t}})$ was taken from the cathode. Two-dimensional spectra of $\Delta E_{3}$ and $E_{\mathrm{r}}$ , gated on $\Delta E_{1}, \Delta E_{2}$ and $E_{\mathrm{t}}$ , were used for ion identification. Two-dimensional spectra for a standard sample $(^{99}\mathrm{Tc}/^{93}\mathrm{Nb}=6.5\times10^{-11})$ and a blank sample are shown in Fig. 1 and Fig. 2, respectively. According to the spectra, $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ and $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ peaks were not fully resolved owing to energy straggling of the ions. In order to improve the separation of $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ and $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ , gates on $\Delta E_{1}, \Delta E_{2}, \Delta E_{3}, E_{\mathrm{r}}$ and $E_{\mathrm{t}}$ were set, which accept 80% of the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ and reject about 80% of $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ ions. In this way, rejection of $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ by up to about 50 times was obtained. A series of standard samples were measured with this method. In total, there are at least 300 counts of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ measured for each standard sample. The results for a single measurement are shown in Fig. 3.

In our case, the $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ contribution to the mass 99 events was subtracted according to the $^{101}\mathrm{Ru}$ counts. The $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ interference was therefore deduced from measurement of $^{101}\mathrm{Ru}$ in the same sample. After normalization, the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ counts were obtained by subtracting the deduced $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ counts from the total count of ions with mass number 99. A blank sample was also measured with this method to check the $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}/^{101}\mathrm{Ru}$ ratio. The measured ratio of $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}/^{101}\mathrm{Ru}$ was 0.80. A series of standard samples were measured with this method and the results are shown in Fig. 4.

![](images/b3abbb238e433c147540442aee49a41ed82990c680dac1ffda2b07728d188378.jpg)

![](images/e7c783d5972d14336b31f39fa8a72739800564877c6a1576484549d530dd1519.jpg)

# 4 Negative Ions of Technetium

According to the negative-ion cookbook $^{[10]}$ , Re, a chemical analog of Tc, has a strong tendency to form $\mathrm{ReC}^{-}$ negative ions. Based on this result, the negative-ion yield of $\mathrm{TcC}^{-}$ also should be high. The negative-ion yield of $\mathrm{TcC}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{Tc}^{-}$ were checked after the AMS methodology of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ was established. A sample with Nb powder as matrix, which was known to have the ratio of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}/^{93}\mathrm{Nb}=1\times 10^{-7}$ and negligible ruthenium, was used. The sample and graphite powder were then mixed in the weight ratio of $1:4$ . This mixture was used to measure the relative yield of $\mathrm{Tc}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{TcC}^{-}$ . The ratio of $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}/^{93}\mathrm{Nb}$ was obtained by measuring the $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ ion counts with the detector and the beam current of $^{93}\mathrm{NbC}^{-}$ with the Faraday cup. Finally, the relative yield of $\mathrm{Tc}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{TcC}^{-}$ was obtained. The relative yield for $\mathrm{Tc}^{-}: \mathrm{TcC}^{-}$ is about $1:25$ (1:1 given in reference [4]). The results, after normalizing to $\mathrm{TcO}^{-}$ , are list in table1.

The relative yields of $\mathrm{Ru}^{-}$ , $\mathrm{RuC}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{RuO}^{-}$ were also measured for comparison. The negative ion spectrum of Ru and its molecular ions is shown in Fig.5. As shown in Fig.5, two kinds of molecular ions are present at mass numbers 112,114 and 116. So, the beam currents at mass numbers $117\left(^{101}\mathrm{Ru}^{16}\mathrm{O}^{-}\right)$ , $118\left(^{102}\mathrm{Ru}^{16}\mathrm{O}^{-}\right)$ and $120\left(^{104}\mathrm{Ru}^{16}\mathrm{O}^{-}\right)$ were used for determining the relative yield of $\mathrm{Ru}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{RuO}^{-}$ . Similarly, the beam currents at mass numbers $108\left(^{96}\mathrm{Ru}^{12}\mathrm{C}^{-}\right)$ , $111\left(^{99}\mathrm{Ru}^{12}\mathrm{C}^{-}\right)$ and $113\left(^{101}\mathrm{Ru}^{12}\mathrm{C}^{-}\right)$ were used for determining the relative yield of $\mathrm{Ru}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{RuC}^{-}$ . The relative yields after normalizing to $\mathrm{RuO}^{-}$ are also shown in table 1.

Table 1 Relative yields of various negative ions of Tc and Ru after independent normalization to $\mathrm{TcO}^{-}$ and $\mathrm{RuO}^{-}$ , respectively

# 5 Results and Discussions

The measurements show that $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ can be measured. The results indicate, for $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}/^{93}\mathrm{Nb}$ ratios in the range $10^{-7}$ to $10^{-10}$ , good linearity of the measured values with the expected values. However, for $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ contents lower than $10^{-11}$ , the measured values were higher than the expected values due to the background of $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ ions or to cross contamination during sample preparation. The detection limit of the method depends sensitively on the $^{99}\mathrm{Ru}$ content in the sample. At present our detection sensitivity is about $1\times 10^8$ atoms. The blank sample measurement showed the content of Ru is about $1\mathrm{mg/g}$ in the $\mathrm{Nb}_2\mathrm{O}_5$ , this is the primary limitation for our high sensitivity $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ measurements. By using high purity chemicals, the sensitivity can be improved further. By extracting $\mathrm{TcC}^-$ , instead of $\mathrm{TcO}^-$ , the background from Ru will be further depressed.

![](images/68e432020d5ad2e46a0e20255114878beffffb1b3e3df48181caf50654f2e3a9.jpg)  
Fig. 5 The negative ion spectrum of Ru and it's molecular ions

# 中文摘要

$^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ 是长寿命放射性裂变核素，通过各种核活动会产生大量的 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ 并排放到环境中。本工作就

是建立利用加速器质谱技术测量 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ 的方法。利用放射性示踪核素建立了适用于AMS测量的化学流程，很好地排除了本底同时高效提取了 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ ，并利用Nb模拟 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ 的稳定同位素，实现了样品的制备。在AMS测量中实现了利用 $^{93}\mathrm{Nb}$ 对 $^{99}\mathrm{Tc}$ 的传输，并利用能量损失和同位素扣除法实现了同量异位素的鉴别，实现了实际环境样品的测定。本工作也研究了Ru和 $\mathrm{Tc}$ 的不同离子引出形式与引出束流的关系，为进一步提高灵敏度提供了方法。

# 2 Study on $^{41}\mathrm{CaF}_2$ AMS Analytical Method and Skeletal Calcium Metabolism by $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ Tracing

Li Shihong, He Ming, Jiang Shan, Dong Kejun, Guan Yongjing,

Wu Shaoyong, Ding Xishen, Yin Xinyi, Yuan Jian

(China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-50, Beijing 102413, China)

The high sensitivity biological $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ tracing and accelerator mass spectrometry (AMS) measurement technique offers a novel way to understand the metabolism of calcium and study the calcium-related disease, which can be used to monitor directly the changes of skeletal metabolism. In these years, studies were carried out as follows.

1) The analytical method of $^{41}\mathrm{Ca} / ^{40}\mathrm{Ca}$ by AMS using $\mathrm{CaF}_2$ target was developed at the Beijing HI-13 AMS system. The biological samples of $\mathrm{CaF}_2$ were prepared with an improved chemical separation and purification procedure. In the $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ AMS measurement, $\mathrm{CaF}_3^-$ negative ion was extracted from ion resource, $7+$ charge state was chosen after carbon foil stripping and accelerated at $8.5\mathrm{MV}$ terminal voltage. $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ ions were finally recorded by a 140 mbar P10 gas filled multi-anode ionization chamber detector. The results showed that $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ and the main isobar, $^{41}\mathrm{K}$ could be clearly identified in the two-dimensional density spectra (Fig.1). The absolutely measured values of 4 standard samples prepared with $^{41}\mathrm{Ca} / ^{40}\mathrm{Ca}$ in the range of $1.785\times 10^{-8} - 1.750\times 10^{-10}$ have a linear relationship (Fig.2). The $^{41}\mathrm{Ca} / ^{40}\mathrm{Ca}$ of the biological blank was estimated below $8.2\times 10^{-13}$ .   
2) $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ tracing plus AMS technique was used for monitoring the change of skeletal metabolism of an osteoporotic rat treated with $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ . Osteoporatic rats were muscularily injected with $0.25~\mathrm{mL}$ of $^{41}\mathrm{CaCl}_2$ solution and effects of $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ treatment on the osteoporosis were evaluated. No significant difference was found of the bone mineral density (BMD) between the $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ groups and the Ovx control group. Results of the micromorphologic observation of the femur slices indicated that the bone resorption was relatively depressed and equilibrated with the bone formation in $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ groups as compared with the Ovx control group. The femur mechanics determination found that a decreasing tendency of bone mechanics characteristics was caused by the long time of $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ administration. The serum alkaline phosphatase (AKP) level in HGa group was lower than that in the control group, implying the $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ administration may also depress the function of bone formation.

![](images/d0ce9fa15ad0a23e93c54c003f871407f37de00cf99967c4ed07aa108982cd12.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Measured two-dimensional spectra $(\Delta E_{1} - \Delta E_{2})$ of standard sample

![](images/275008088858d457f2be90ca5aea7220cebd8171fa5f1ce16f1ddb1ed290fe07.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Results from series standard samples

It was found that the $^{41}\mathrm{Ca} / \mathrm{Ca}$ ratios of the feces decreased rapidly with increasing time after the Ovx and Sham rats injected with $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ and then slowly approximately after one month, suggesting at least two mechanisms of $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ metabolism with different speeds may be present. Urines were used as the reference material of interior calcium excretion and the parameters of bioavailability of calcium were calculated. The ratio of $^{41}\mathrm{Ca} / \mathrm{Ca}$ of feces to that of urine, which represented the quotient of interior calcium was only in the range of $1\% -3\%$ .

3) To explore the usage of $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ tracer for studying the skeleton resorption function in vitro, the effect of $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ on the resorption of osteoclasts was evaluated by $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ tracing. Osteoclasts were incubated with synthesized $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ -labeled hydroxyapatite particles and $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ of 0, 5, 10, 20 μmol/L. The medium and osteoclasts in each culture were isolated on day 1, 2 and 7, respectively and were measured with AMS. It was found that $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}/\mathrm{Ca}$ of the osteoclasts on day 1 was higher than those on day 2 and 7, which were comparable. However, the $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}/\mathrm{Ca}$ of the media was lower on day 1 than those on day 2 and 7, which

were also comparable. Those results showed that osteoclasts mainly absorbed calcium from the bone fragments other than from medium on day 1, meanwhile exchange of calcium with the media was relatively weak, subsequently, the Ca absorbing ability of osteoclasts from the bone fragments decreased and the exchange ability with the media increased. $^{41}$ Ca/Ca of osteoclasts on day 1 and 2 with $20\mu \mathrm{mol} / \mathrm{L}$ $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ presented in the media were lower than that without $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ . $^{41}$ Ca/Ca of media containing $20\mu \mathrm{mol} / \mathrm{L}$ $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ on day 2 and 7 were also lower than that of medium containing no $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ . The low bone calcium resorption caused by $20\mu \mathrm{mol} / \mathrm{L}$ $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ presented in the media implies $\mathrm{GaCl}_3$ may depress the bone resorption function of osteoclasts.

Though the total calcium content of the osteoclasts was much lower than that of the medium, the $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ content in taken by the osteoclasts was higher than that dispersed in the medium, especially on day 1 and 2, suggesting that osteoclasts have strong bone resorption ability, whose calcium intake speed is higher than the exchange speed of dissolved calcium from the bone fragments with that in the medium.

# 中文摘要

为开展 $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ 的生物示踪应用工作，建立了样品形式为 $\mathrm{CaF}_2$ 的样品制备流程和AMS测量技术，在此基础上开展了利用 $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ 示踪研究骨钙的代谢行为，分别利用 $^{41}\mathrm{Ca}$ 对大鼠和细胞进行了示踪研究。对于老鼠的示踪是研究镓盐对骨质疏松大鼠钙代谢的影响，而对细胞的示踪研究就是研究不同浓度的镓盐对破骨细胞钙吸收的影响。

# 3 Measurement of Trace $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ Concentrations in Neutrino Detector Material With Accelerator Mass Spectrometry

Dong Kejun<sup>1</sup>, He Ming<sup>1</sup>, Jiang Shan<sup>1</sup>, Wong Henry Tsz-king<sup>2</sup>, Qiu Jiuzi<sup>1,3</sup>, Guan Yongjing<sup>1</sup>, Wu Shaoyong<sup>1</sup>, Li Shihong<sup>1</sup>, Bao Yiwen<sup>1</sup>, You Qubo<sup>1</sup>, Lin Min<sup>1</sup>, Yin Xinyi<sup>1</sup>, Zhou Duo<sup>1</sup>, Hu Yueming<sup>1</sup>, Yuan Jian<sup>1</sup>

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, Beijing 102413, China;   
2. Institute of Physics Academia Sinica, Taibei 11529, China;   
3. Armed Police Force Academy, Langfang 065000, China)

Measurement of trace concentrations of naturally-occurring and cosmic-ray induced radioactive isotopes has an important impact to low background experiments, such as those for Dark Matter searches, as well as the studies of double beta decay, reactor and solar neutrinos[1]. The isotope $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ is a long-lived (half-life $1.57\times 10^{7}$ years) fission product with yields of $0.74\%$ and $1.5\%$ for thermal neutron-induced fission of $^{235}\mathrm{U}$ and $^{239}\mathrm{Pu}$ , respectively. It is commonly found in the environment, iodine being readily soluble in water. The isotope 129I decays via

$$
\begin{array}{l} { } ^ { 1 2 9 } \mathrm { I } \rightarrow { } ^ { 1 2 9 } \mathrm { X e } ^ { * } + \mathrm { e } ^ { - } ( T _ { 1 / 2 } = 1 . 5 7 \times 1 0 ^ { 7 } \mathrm { a } , Q _ { \beta } = 1 9 4 \mathrm { k e V } ) \\ { } ^ { 1 2 9 } \mathrm { X e } ^ { * } \rightarrow { } ^ { 1 2 9 } \mathrm { X e } + \gamma \left( T _ { 1 / 2 } = 0 . 9 7 \mu \mathrm { s } , E _ { \gamma } = 3 9 . 6 \mathrm { k e V } \right) \\ \end{array}
$$

Such processes can contribute to the background in Dark Matter and low energy neutrino

experiments, such as the measurement of neutrino magnetic moment[1].

An organic liquid and an inorganic salt were selected since they require different experimental procedures and systematic effect considerations. The organic liquid studied is the standard mixture of mesitylene (1, 3, 5-trimethylbenzene) and PPO liquid scintillator (LS). The processing and measurement procedures with other organic solvent and dyes are expected to be very similar. The inorganic salt selected was CsI powder, since CsI(Tl) crystal scintillators are being used in reactor neutrino and dark matter experiments[2,3].

No chemical procedures are necessary for the CsI powder, which was directly used in the AMS measurement. However, CsI is a hygroscopic material, which can be easily led to injector magnet excursion. Accordingly, the CsI samples were deposited quickly onto a cathode of electrolytic copper in a dry box. The cathode was then baked in an oven at $100^{\circ}\mathrm{C}$ for two days prior to the measurement. For the organic LS, an extraction procedure for the possible iodine contaminations has to be devised. The adopted sample preparation procedure is shown schematically in Fig.1.

The $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ concentration in the CsI powder and the LS extracted in the form of AgI were measured with CIAE-AMS facility. Particle detection and identification of $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ was performed via TOF (time-of-flight) detector. A detailed layout of the TOF system is shown in Fig.2. Standard AgI samples with $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ were prepared and verified by the procedures described in reference [4]. According to the contrast results, an uncertainty better than $1\%$ for the $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ source strength in the samples could be obtained.

![](images/6303ccc51cb1a863451edda8e66494e64444f4cea9bb3237e141a381e3f80417.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Flow chart of samples preparation for organic liquid scintillator

The result shows the sensitivity of $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ detection is $5 \times 10^{-13}$ . The limit is directly applicable to characterize the $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ contaminations in CsI and LS. It can be alternatively expressed as $< 6 \times 10^{-13} \, \mathrm{g/g}$ for

CsI and $2.6 \times 10^{-17} \mathrm{~g/g}$ for LS, respectively.

![](images/77ee79296a50714b586c3ad0b3aea4dda88bcbc7c0d579e332742fb2ec104f84.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Schematic layout of TOF
