# 中文摘要

测量天然与宇宙射线诱发产生的微量放射性核素 $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ 的浓度对于低本底试验是一项非常重要的技术。试验选用常用的中微子探测器材料 CsI 晶体与有机液体闪烁体(LS)作为研究对象。对于 CsI 晶体，AMS 可直接测量。而对于 LS 样品，试验采用低压分馏、加入碘化物载体、萃取与反萃等流程把 LS 中的待测核素提取出来。并最终转化成适合于 AMS 测量的 AgI 样品形式。 $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ 的测量采用目前已经建立的 AMS-TOF 方法。试验的初步结果表明：CsI 中 $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ 含量小于 $6 \times 10^{-13} \mathrm{~g/g}$ ，液体闪烁体中 $^{129}\mathrm{I}$ 含量小于 $2.6 \times 10^{-17} \mathrm{~g/g}$ 。

# 4 Measurements Method of $^{151}\mathrm{Sm}$ and $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ With AMS

Yin Xinyi $^{1}$ , Wang Wei $^{1}$ , Wang Youzhou $^{1,2}$ , He Ming $^{1}$ , Dong Kejun $^{1}$ , Guan Yongjing $^{1}$ , Wu Shaoyong $^{1}$ , Bao Yiwen $^{1}$ , Yuan Jian $^{1}$ , Jiang Shan $^{1}$

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-50, Beijing 102413, China;

2. School of Physical Science and Technology, Lanzhou University, Lanzhou 730000, China)

$^{151}\mathrm{Sm}$ and $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ are long-lived nuclides. They have very important applications in industry, agriculture and national defense etc. But the contents of Sm and Se in samples are very low especially in biological

samples. It is difficult to detect trace amount of $^{151}\mathrm{Sm}$ and $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ , so accelerator mass spectrometry (AMS) is the best choice for the measurement of Sm and Se with high sensitivity.

The rare earth Sm can deteriorate the ionization efficiency of the ionizer because of its characteristics such as low vapor pressure and low work function. The phenomenon is called the ionizer poisoning, which causes the rapid current decrease. So resolving the problem of ionizer poisoning is the key factor for $^{151}\mathrm{Sm}$ AMS measurement.

For the cesium sputter ion source, the ionization energy of Cs (3.89 eV) is lower than the work function of molybdenum (4.6 eV) as the ionizer element. Therefore the most outer electron of Cs easily transfers to ionizer, and ionization efficiency is almost 100% at the surface temperature of over 1000 °C. Negative ions and neutral elements are ejected from the cathode by $\mathrm{Cs^{+}}$ sputtering, then are extracted with a puller electrode. Sputtered neutral Sm atoms that stick to the ionizer deteriorate its ionization efficiency because the work function of Sm (2.7 eV) is lower than the ionization energy of Cs (3.89 eV). The ionizer can't operate stably for a long time due to, the ionizer poisoning[1].

We have resolved the problem by using tungsten cathode. The design is based on two ideas: 1) The ionizer surface should be free from being exposed to recoil samarium by covering the cathode surface with tungsten; 2) The solid angle formed by the cathode hole is almost equal to that of the center hole of the ionizer from the cathode. Thus we could control the emission angle from the cathode to the ionizer and restrict the poisoning effect.

As a result, damage to the ionizer decreased, and stable operation of the ion source was successfully achieved with a $\mathrm{SmO}^{-}$ beam current of $105\mathrm{nA}$ at CIAE HI-13 tandem AMS facility. And we can make a primary conclusion based on the experimental resultsthat $\mathrm{Sm}_2\mathrm{O}_3$ performed well. Moreover, Eu suppression can be achieved using $\mathrm{SmO}^{-}$ ions. So $\mathrm{Sm}_2\mathrm{O}_3$ may be the proper chemical form of Sm for the AMS measurement.

As for $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ , CdSe and AgBr mixed with Carbon powder were filled in the cathodes, and different negative ions are extracted from the source. The experimental results show that, the beam intensity of $\mathrm{SeC}_2^-$ is up to 70 nA, at the same time the beam current of $\mathrm{BrC}_2^-$ is very weak (about 0.7 nA). So CdSe may be the best chemical form for AMS measurement.

There are still some work to be done in the near future.

# Reference

[1] Saitoh Y, Yotsombat B, Mizuhashi K, et al. Production of Ce negative ions in a Cs sputter ion source. Review of Science Instruments, 2000, V71(2): 955-957.

# 中文摘要

采用加速器质谱(AMS)对 $^{151}\mathrm{Sm}$ 和 $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ 进行精确测量，旨在建立与发展用于分子水平环境毒理学研究的核分析方法。采用钨(W)锥，W粉做导电物质，样品在锥中的深度为 $2\mathrm{mm}$ ，解决了离子源中毒问题。经多次实验样品形式确定为 $\mathrm{Sm}_2\mathrm{O}_3$ ，引出 $\mathrm{SmO}^-$ ，束流大而且稳定(约 $100\mathrm{nA}$ )，并且完成了标准样品的研制。对于 $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ ，样品形式为CdSe，C粉做导电物质，引出 $\mathrm{SeC}_2^-(70\mathrm{nA})$ 。测量中存在 $^{79}\mathrm{Br}$ 的干扰，难点是 $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ 的鉴别，下一步将建立充气磁谱仪来实现 $^{79}\mathrm{Se}$ 的探测和鉴别。

# 5 The Primary Study of Radiation Effect of Biology Induced by Protons

Yue Maoxing $^{1}$ , Liu Zhiguo $^{1}$ , Li Jianzhong $^{1}$ , Xu Bingxin $^{1}$ , Chen Ying $^{2}$ , Luo Qingliang $^{2}$ , Chen Axin $^{2}$ , Xiao Min $^{2}$ , Jing Hua $^{2}$ , Hua Nan $^{2}$ ,

Zhao Kui<sup>3</sup>, Sui Li<sup>3</sup>, Guo Jiyu<sup>3</sup>, Kong Fuquan<sup>3</sup>, Cai Minghui<sup>3</sup>, Liu Jiancheng<sup>3</sup>

(1. Special Medicine of Center of the 306th Hospital PLA, Beijing 100101, China;   
2. Institute of Radiation Medicine, Beijing 100850, China;   
3. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

Radiation in outspace environment is a serious threat to astronauts for long space flights. Galactic cosmic rays (GCR) and solar particle events (SPE) are the most important space radiation sources. Both the GCR and the SPE contain significant number of high-energy protons capable of large penetration and important nuclear interaction. Those protons have high linear energy transfer and higher relative biological effectiveness (RBE), compared with the low LET radiation (such as electrons, X-rays and $\gamma$ -rays) and thus can induce greater damage to biological specimen. Shielding method can not prevent all of radiation risk, radioprotector is an essential requirement for astronauts.

In order to provide basic data for the radioprotector the protons accelerated by HI-13 tandem accelerator are used to simulate the space environment and the primary experiment about radiobiological effect induced by protons is performed. The protons of 19 MeV bombard the gold target of $1.3\mathrm{mg/cm}^2$ , scattered protons are defocus by Q3D spectrometer and then go through a Kapton foil of $50~\mu \mathrm{m}$ , the air of $1.5\mathrm{cm}$ and the plastic foil of $0.1\mathrm{mm}$ . Energy of protons arriving at the surface of samples is $18\mathrm{MeV}$ . The LET is about $2.9\mathrm{keV}/\mu \mathrm{m}$ with a range of $3.5\mathrm{mm}$ in water.

An Au-Si surface barrier semi-conductor detector is fixed at $35^{\circ}$ in the target chamber as the fluence monitor of protons. The plasmid DNA, human blood, marrow of monkey and ICR mouse are irradiated by protons in the dose range of 0-30 Gy.

The primary experimental results are as follows.

1) There are no obvious changes in the AFM images of plasmid DNA up to 30 Gy proton radiation.   
2) The indexes of human blood are examined for 7 days after proton radiation. The results show that chromosomal aberration fractions of human peripheral blood cell are higher at dose of 0.5, 1 and 3 Gy, then descend at 6 Gy and chromosomal aberration not observed at the dose of 10 Gy, but vacuoles appear in the cells. That is a sign of cell apoptosis.   
3) The cell cycle measurement indicates that serious G0/G1 arrest take place in cells of monkey marrow after proton radiation.   
4) The radiobiology effects in mouse induce by protons are various. The depilation phenomena appear at first. Fig.1 shows the depilation phenomena of mouse induced by proton radiation at different doses. On the 14th days after proton radiation survive fraction measurements of mouse indicate that mice survive by $100\%$ when they suffer from proton radiation of 4 and 8 Gy, and $80\%$ at dose of 16 Gy. When dose increases to 32 Gy, all of mice died. Besides the depilation and burning of skin, some mutations present in the skin cells as well as tissues of hart, liver, lung and lung of mouse. Fig.2 indicates the viscera

changes on the 16th days after proton radiation at 16 Gy. Fig.2a shows the scarfskin discontinuity and break and the decrease of fair follicle as well as edema. A liquefaction putrescence range appears on the center of the hart tissue in Fig.2b. Three liquefaction putrescence ranges and bleeding of liver cell are visible in Fig.2c. Fig.2d indicates the hyperemia occurs slightly in lung and alveolus gaps become thick.

![](images/ae4a6b98f959affde2c7dae4cbab35e37b985641bc8c7591e51406490caa9259.jpg)  
Fig. 1 The depilation phenomena of mouse induced by proton radiation at different doses

![](images/854fbdcabb81be91e67ed78e4e5be9de87f9f1bac406ca12f1a153ce8df9b74e.jpg)  
Fig. 2 The viscera changes on the 16th days after proton radiation at 16 Gy

# 中文摘要

尝试利用加速器模拟空间质子辐射环境，进行质子致生物效应实验研究，以对高效低副作用的抗质子辐射防护剂的研制和筛选提供基础数据。实验是在HI-13串列加速器上进行的，质子的入射能量为 $19 \mathrm{MeV}$ ，到达样品表面的能量为 $18.0 \mathrm{MeV}$ ，在水中的LET值约 $2.9 \mathrm{keV} / \mu \mathrm{m}$ ，射程约为 $3.5 \mathrm{~mm}$ 。以 $0 \sim 30 \mathrm{Gy}$ 间的不同剂量分别对质粒DNA、猴子骨髓细胞、人血细胞和小鼠等生物样品进行了辐照。实验结果报告了经质子辐照后各种样品随剂量的变化情况。

# 6 Investigation of DNA Damage Induced by Direct and Indirect Effects of $^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ Ions Radiation

Sui Li, Zhao Kui, Ni Meinan, Guo Jiyu, Kong Fuquan,

Cai Minghui, Yang Mingjian, Liu Jiancheng

(China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

There are Numerous DNA lesions, such as base damage, single- and double- strand breaks (SSBs and DSBs), and cross-linking induced by direct and indirect effects of ionizing radiation. The DSBs of DNA are considered to be the most important initial damage of all biological effects induced by radiation. Free radical produced by radiolysis of water may be the main factor of yielding SSBs and DSBs in aqueous solution[1-4]. It is essential to study the dynamics of free radical reaction by establishing proper in vitro model systems. Heavy ion radiation is high linear energy transfer (LET) radiation, producing more severe damage than low LET radiation resulting in the increase of cell death. In this study, the mechanism of DNA strand breaks induced by $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ions are investigated with atomic force microscopy (AFM), which can offer the data for cancer therapy by heavy ion radiation method and the dangerous assessment of space or nuclear radiation, etc.

In the experiment, using $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ions of 19 MeV (LET=120 keV/μm, in aqueous solution) generated by HI-13 tandem accelerator, three pUC19 plasmid DNA samples including dry, aqui-form and adding various concentrations radical scavenger (mannitol, aqueous solution) are irradiated at different doses from 10 Gy to 1 000 Gy in air. After irradiating, these DNA samples are analyzed with AJ-III AFM in nanometer-scale. The changes of supercoiled (intact DNA), open circular (OC, DNA with SSB) and linear form (L, DNA with DSB) are observed.

For the dry DNA, the fraction of OC form increases with increasing dose and is larger than that for the aqui-form DNA under the dose of 700 Gy. But the fraction of L form shows irregular change as the dose increases, and far lower than that for the aqui-form DNA. These imply that the direct effect may be the main reason for yielding SSBs in dry DNA and the indirect effect may be the main factor of yielding DSBs in aqueous solution. Furthermore, DNA-DNA cross-linking is observed in irradiated dry DNA, which competes with DNA strand breaks. This may be a reason of lower yield of DSBs in dry DNA.

For the aqui-form DNA, the forms of DNA show regular change from SC to OC or L as the dose increases. At a dose of 1000 Gy, nearly $90\%$ of the DNA molecules are in long or short linear fragments. The representative AFM image is presented in Fig.1.

For the DNA samples in aqueous solution added with various concentrations of mannitol, the stronger protection effect of mannitol for DNA molecules is observed at a dose of 1000 Gy. As the concentrations of mannitol increase, the fraction of SC form increases, but the fraction of L form decreases gradually. At the 400 mmol of concentration, the percent of L form is only $5\%$ . The representative AFM image is shown in Fig.2. These indicate that mannitol can effectively scavenge free radical and reduce the yields of SSB and DSB. These results also reveal that free radical is the important factor in ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ ion radiation induced DNA SSBs and DSBs under condition of aqueous solution.

![](images/405bdec4d914d836beacae4e7b805cdf51cb2ad4d9c9f36e733185a9dab77677.jpg)  
Fig. 1 AFM image of pure DNA irradiated with $^7\mathrm{Li}$ ions of 1000 Gy

![](images/3a1370d346cd251422e2c7183ebe1432922e6d7ca51f52e11ebae556050b81a7.jpg)  
Fig. 2 AFM image of DNA with 400 mmol mannitol of irradiated with $^7$ Li ions of 1000 Gy

![](images/921391a4c9996309b118696429068933a4aa29f9a3b0e59293b1814c8b1074d2.jpg)  
Fig. 3 Histogram of the three forms of dry DNA after $\gamma$ -ray and $^7$ Li ion irradiations

In order to estimate the relative biological effectiveness (RBE) of $^7 \mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion radiation, three pUC19 plasmid DNA samples are irradiated by $^{60}\mathrm{Co}$ $\gamma$ -rays at various doses from 500 to 2 000 Gy. The result analyzed with AFM shows that $\gamma$ -rays more severely induce DNA strand breaks in condition of aqueous solution compared with dry. That is to say free radical may be the main factor in DNA damage induced by $\gamma$ -ray radiation. Fractions of three forms of dry DNA after $^7 \mathrm{Li}$ ion and $\gamma$ -ray irradiations are compared. Histogram of dry DNA after $\gamma$ -ray and $^7 \mathrm{Li}$ ion irradiations is shown in Fig.3. This indicates that direct effect is important in DNA damage induced by $^7 \mathrm{Li}$ ion radiation.

# References

[1] Siddiqi M A, et al. Radiat Res, 1987, 112: 449-463.   
[2] Shao C L, et al. Acta Laser Biology Sinica, 1999, 8(2): 97-101.   
[3] Shao C L, et al. Acta Biochimica et Biophysica Sinica, 2000, 32(4): 379-382.   
[4] Rajagopalan R, et al. J Radiat Res, 2002, 43: 153-159.

# 中文摘要

本实验利用HI-13串列加速器加速的 $19\mathrm{MeV}(\mathrm{LET}$ 约为 $120\mathrm{keV} / \mu \mathrm{m})$ 的 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 重离子，在大气环境下，以 $10\sim 1000\mathrm{Gy}$ 的不同剂量分别对水状、干状（目的是排除自由基间接作用的影响）和含不同浓度自由基清除剂甘露醇的pUC19质粒DNA进行了辐照。之后，使用原子力显微镜(AFM)研究了重离子辐射中直接和间接作用对DNA链断裂的影响。结果表明：对于水溶液DNA，具有较高LET的 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 离子可诱发DNA分子发生集团损伤，即形成更多和更短的DNA小碎片，并使DSB的分布更局部和更密集；干状DNA和自由基清除剂对DNA链断裂的影响显示，在水溶液环境下，自由基是诱发DNA链断裂的主导因素；甘露醇可有效地清除自由基，降低双链断裂的产额，具有一定的辐射防护作用。这些结果可为核和空间辐射环境下机体的辐射损伤与防护及重离子治癌等研究提供必要的基础数据。

# 7 DNA End-Joining Catalyzed by Cell-Free Extracts After Heavy Ions Irradiation*

Kong Fuquan<sup>1</sup>, Zhao Kui<sup>1</sup>, Zhou Pingkun<sup>2</sup>, Ni Meinan<sup>1</sup>, Sui Li<sup>1</sup>, Cai Minghui<sup>1</sup>

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China;

2. Department of Radiation Toxicology, Institute of Radiation Medicine,

Academy of Military Medical Sciences, Beijing 100850, China)

DNA is considered to be the most important bio-macromolecule and target molecule responsible for all biological effects. Many kinds of damage can be induced by radiation, for example, base damage, single strand break (ssb), double strand break (dsb) and crosslink of DNA and protein[1,2]. DNA double strand breaks (DSBs) are considered to be the most important initial damage of all biological effects induced by high-LET heavy ion radiation. In particular, unrejoined DSBs are responsible for cell death. It

is inductability that the damage to astronaut induced by heavy ions in outer space. So the experiment of DNA end-joining catalyzed by cell-free extracts after DNA damaged by heavy ions is done.

The DNA sample is pGEM-T1 double-stranded plasmid DNA and is offered by department of radiation toxicology of Beijing institute of radiation medicine, Academy of Military Medical Sciences. The original forms of the DNA sample are a mixture of supercoiled $(80\%)$ and open circular type. In the experiment, $19\mathrm{MeV}^7$ Li heavy ions (LET=120 keV/μm, in water) are generated by HI-13 tandem accelerator at China Institute of Atomic Energy (CIAE), and the DNA samples in aqueous solution are irradiated with various doses (4, 6 and 8 Gy) in atmosphere at room temperature. After irradiation, DNA end-joining is catalyzed by cell-free extracts in vitro system. Fig.1 shows the result of agarose gels electrophoresis.

![](images/305680567f958f0deadab75bf1e4d866632af2aaaf11e7b822b45029d0b4cc37.jpg)

![](images/c319f12e8dd309a8442b9a191779ab5ea7518ede5c1e28a30d75b4a3e16f94c5.jpg)  
Fig.1 DNA end-joining after irradiation by heavy ions $^7\mathrm{Li}$ a—Cell-free extracts is $18.8~\mu \mathrm{g}$ b—Cell-free extracts is 63.45

In Fig. 1a and b, M is marker; 4, 6 and 8 are DNA irradiated with 4, 6 and 8 Gy respectively; $4^{\prime}$ , $6^{\prime}$ and $8^{\prime}$ are DNAs that are end-joined after ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion irradiatingons with doses of 4, 6 and 8 Gy, respectively.

In agarose gels electrophoresis, the supercoiled DNA moves more quickly than the linear form DNA because the supercoiled DNA is compact. In Fig.1, the dimer form DNAs pointed by Peter Baumann[3] do not appear. But the fraction of linear form DNA increases after catalyzing of cell-free extract, which is contrast to that before catalyzing. This phenomenon is more evident in the dose of 6 Gy than that in other doses. The reaction system B has more cell-free extracts than the reaction A. So it is evident that the increasing of linear form DNA in reaction system B is more than that in reaction system A. It may be that there are some damages of bases and sugar-bases in the DNA that still keep up the supercoiled form after irradiation. In the cell-free extracts, there may have some enzymes that can distinguish these damages and cut out them. Thus the supercoiled DNA turns into linear form DNA. The dimer and trimer do not appear in the experiment. The reason may be that two ends of linear DNA induced by heavy ions are not like enzyme cut. All of these need more work.

[3] Peter B, Stephen C. West DNA end-joining catalyzed by human cell-free extracts. 1998, 95: 14066-14070.

# 中文摘要

DNA 作为生物体中的一类基本生物大分子，是离子辐射引致生物效应的关键靶分子。因此，我们进行了重离子 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 引起的质粒 DNA 损伤的体外修复实验。本文进行了修复实验中最初步和基础的 DNA 碎片的末端连接实验。结果表明，经过催化后的 DNA 中并没有出现 Peter Baumann 等实验中出现的二聚体，但修复后的条带与修复前的相比较，线性的比例明显增加，在 B 中 6Gy 时表现的更为明显一些。并且 B 中线性 DNA 的增加比 A 中较为明显，原因为 B 中无细胞提取物比 A 中的多。

# 8 Bio-effects of $^7$ Li Ion Beams Irradiation on Wheat*

Liu Luxiang $^{1}$ , Guo Huijun $^{1}$ , Zhao Linshu $^{1}$ , Zhao Kui $^{2}$ , Zhao Shirong $^{1}$ , Sui Li $^{2}$ , Kong Fuquan $^{2}$

(1. Key Laboratory of Agricultural Nuclear Technology and Space Breeding, Ministry of Agriculture, Institute of Crop Science, Chinese Academy of Agricultural Sciences, Beijing 100081, China;

2. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China)

Exploration of new mutagens is of importance for upgrading mutagenic efficiency in plant breeding. Low LET radiation such as X- and $\gamma$ -rays, as well as chemicals, has been applied to expand genetic resources in plants so far. Recent accelerator beams promote the applicability of high-LET radiation to improve plant varieties. At present, limited data are available on the scope of genetic effects at the phenotypic and molecular level in crops. This study aims at determining types, frequencies, rates and patterns of phenotypic and genotypic changes induced by gamma rays and $^7$ Li ion beams used for wheat improvement. The progress in the first two years is reported as following.

Air-drying seeds of the wheat cultivars ZH7 and ZY9 were irradiated by using heavy ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam generated by tandem accelerator at the energy of $42.3\mathrm{MeV}$ with dosage of 30, 50, 100 and $150\mathrm{Gy}$ , respectively. The gamma rays with the same dosages were used as control. Germination test was conducted at constant temperature of $21~^\circ \mathrm{C}$ with treated and non-treated seeds at the same time. 100 seeds in each treatment and two repetitions for each treatment were set. Germination potential and rate were investigated and seedling height and root length after 10 days were measured. Optimal dosage was determined through the relationship between bio-effect-dosage of heavy ion beams. $\mathbf{M}_2$ populations of ZY9 and ZH7 were planted in the experiment station of the Institute of Crop Science of Chinese Academy of Agricultural Sciences by using $\mathbf{M}_1$ seeds.

Biological effect studies showed that $^7\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam implantation inhibited the germination rate, seedling height and root length in $\mathbf{M}_1$ generation. There was no linear dose-effect relationship but the

"Braag peak" effect in $^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam implantation with comparison of that of traditional gamma ray radiation. It was found 50 Gy could be the proper dose of $^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam to irradiate wheat seeds at the energy of 42.3 MeV. Compared with the gamma ray radiation, $^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam implantation induced relatively lighter biological damage effects. Various morphological and cytological aberrations of the seedlings occurred. The most significant variations were chlorophyll deficiency of main vein, leaf split, leaf curl and tufted seedling.

It was found that $^7\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam implantation into crop seeds can produce the effects of not only the energy transferring, mass deposition and charge exchange as the same as the other ion beams, but also the reaction of $^1\mathrm{H}(^7\mathrm{Li}, ^7\mathrm{Be})$ n in the irradiated seeds. Above results showed 50 Gy could be the proper dose of $^7\mathrm{Li}$ ion beam to irradiate wheat seeds at the energy of 42.3 MeV.

Analysis of mutation types and frequencies of $\mathbf{M}_2$ populations showed that $^7\mathrm{Li}$ ion beams could induce significant wheat mutations. Mutation phenotypes of $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion beams were mainly spike types and plant height. Since the very limited $\mathbf{M}_2$ population size of $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion treatments, the statistics of frequency of mutations and mutants was incomplete. However, the results that 50 Gy of $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion treatment could produce the highest mutation in both frequency and types, further revealed that 50 Gy could be the optimal irradiation dose for $^7\mathrm{Li}$ heavy ion beam treatment in wheat.

# 中文摘要

本研究利用中国原子能科学研究院HI-13串列加速器产生的能量为 $43 \mathrm{MeV}$ , LET为 $60 \mathrm{keV} / \mu \mathrm{m}$ 的 ${ }^{7} \mathrm{Li}$ 离子束辐照处理两个冬小麦品种ZH7和ZY9的风干种子,剂量分别为30、50、100和150Gy,并以相同剂量的 $\gamma$ 射线作为对照。试验结果表明, ${ }^{7} \mathrm{Li}$ 离子束处理可明显抑制小麦幼苗生长,但剂量和苗高之间并不成线性关系,其中,50Gy时损伤效应最大; ${ }^{7} \mathrm{Li}$ 离子束注入可产生高频率的双生苗、叶脉失绿和叶片开裂、卷曲、畸形等多种形态变化,进而引起小麦 $\mathbf{M}_{2}$ 代在株高、穗型、熟期、育性和干粒重等多种性状的变异,是一种可以应用于作物突变育种的新诱变因素。

# 12 Identification of SEU Sensitive Region of CMOS SRAM Using Heavy Ion Microbeam

Guo Gang $^{1}$ , Hui Ning $^{1}$ , Chen Quan $^{1}$ , Shen Dongjun $^{1}$ , Xu Jincheng $^{1}$ , He Chaohui $^{2}$ , Guo Hongxia $^{2}$ , Li Yonghong $^{2}$ , Luo Yinhong $^{2}$ , Yao Zhibin $^{2}$ , Zhang Fengqi $^{2}$ , Lu Hong $^{3}$ , Su Xiudi $^{3}$ , Li Wei $^{3}$

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-10, Beijing 102413, China;   
2. Northwest Institute of Nuclear Technology, P. O. Box 69-1, Xi'an 710024, China;   
3. Northeast Microelectronics Institute, Shenyang 110032, China)

Single-event upset (SEU) occurs in storage elements when the charge collected from a heavy-ion interaction exceeds the critical charge required to upset the circuit. The circuit then changes state, and stored information is lost. The circuit can be restored to its original operating state by rewriting or reinitializing the circuit.

In early 2005, a 2 K-bit static random access memory (SRAM) was irradiated using $^{79}\mathrm{Br}$ ion beam with energy of $145\mathrm{MeV}$ at the heavy-ion microbeam facility connected with HI-13 tandem accelerator at China Institute of Atomic Energy (CIAE). In this experiment, the SRAM with the radiation-hardened design was provided by Northeast Microelectronics Institute (NMI).

The energy spectrum of $^{79}\mathrm{Br}$ ions in back of pinhole was measured before experiment, and the beam spot of the used microbeam was then estimated to be around $2.5\mu \mathrm{m} \times 3.5\mu \mathrm{m}$ accordingly. The microbeam scanning was performed over four cells of SRAM with a beam dwell time of 5 s (about 15 ions). The area scanned for each SEU image was $80\mu \mathrm{m} \times 80\mu \mathrm{m}$ with a step size of $3\mu \mathrm{m}$ . Before irradiation, initial states of cell were in "0" or "1" state.

SEU signals were monitored with a SEU tester provided by Northwest Institute of Nuclear Technology (NINT). The beam was closed by beam shutter when the change of cell state was detected or the preset time was achieved. After the data of the state of cell together with the irradiated position were written down, the SRAM was moved to the next position. Such process was repeated over the entire scan area.

The SEU sensitive regions and the areas of the SRAM cell were then identified using above technique (so-called SEU mapping) at the first time in China. Fig. 1 is the microscope image of surface of SRAM cell. And the Fig. 2 is the SEU-image of the 2 K-bit CMOS SRAM. The black solid squares in Fig.2 indicate the changes from "0" to "1" of the bit state, the red solid circles indicate changes from "1"

to “0” of the bit state, and the blue stars show the two upset. The characters in the figure denote the site where the state of cell is changed.

The Fig.2 indicates the existence of two SEU-sensitive regions per cell with different area. After comparing the SEU image with the cell design, it is found that the SEU-sensitive regions are the reversed-biased NMOS and PMOS drain, which are in the "off"-state. Measuring the areas of the SEU-sensitive region gives $(58\pm 32)\mu \mathrm{m}^2$ for NMOS, $(14\pm 10)\mu \mathrm{m}^2$ for PMOS, and a total sensitive area of $(71\pm 34)\mu \mathrm{m}^2$ per cell. It is less than one-tenth of the total cell area, which is $35\mu \mathrm{m}\times 25\mu \mathrm{m}$ .

![](images/02abc66f766aa6a351e7ef10d32b2d823613091df31b30ebf742ab54d07e16ff.jpg)  
Fig.1 The microscope image of SRAM cell

![](images/aec97113a01806ef18e5637b93a0b948506c5723c752e2a1bddfc4c614ee3657.jpg)  
Fig. 2 The SEU-image of a 2 K-bit CMOS SRAM

# 中文摘要

在 HI-13 串列加速器重离子微束单粒子效应辐射装置上，使用入射能量为 $145 \mathrm{MeV}$ 的 ${ }^{79} \mathrm{Br}$ 离子，经 $2 \mu \mathrm{m} \times 3 \mu \mathrm{m}$ 的针孔准直后，辐照存储容量为 $2 \mathrm{~K}$ 的静态随机存储器(SRAM)，通过测量单粒子翻转(SEU)与入射束流位置之间的关系(即SEU成像技术)，确定了该存储器存储单元内部的SEU敏感区域的位置和面积。

# 13 Study on Properties of Solid Material Surface Modified by Nuclear Tracks

Liu Cunxiong, Ni Bangfa, Xiao Caijin, Tian Weizhi, Wang Pingsheng, Zhang Guiying, Huang Donghui, Li Dehong, Li Xuezhen, Shen Li, Yang Weitao (China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-50, Beijing 102413, China)

Study on peculiar properties of material surfaces modified by nuclear tracks was originated from the solid state nuclear track detection techniques developed half century ago for recording and investigating the properties of various nuclear particles. In this work, the nuclear tracks produced in solid materials were used to study insulating and optical characteristics of some nuclear track modified solid materials. Some preliminary conclusions have been achieved.

The insulating materials were used in experiments because only in insulating materials nuclear tracks can be recorded. Polyester, polycarbonate and mica were selected as test samples. Polyester and polycarbonate belong to organic macromolecule polymer, mica is inorganic. The sensitivity to record nuclear tracks and the method to develop the tracks are all different for polymer and mica. Sensitivity of polymer is much greater than mica. Alkaline solution was used to etch samples to make tracks developed, for mica strong acid is appropriate. In our experiments sodium hydroxide and hydrofluoric acid were selected as the etching solution for polymers and mica, respectively. The etching conditions (density of tracks, concentration and temperature of etching solution, time for etching samples) were varied to meet different requirements on the diameter, the depth (and less importantly, the shape) of the tracks on sample surfaces. As a result, properties of nuclear track modified sample surface were changed. And it varied with different diameter and depth. The electric resistance and optical parameters such as reflectivity, transmission were measured. Fig. 1 shows two SEM pictures for polyester surface. The Fig. 1a shows excellent anti-metal contamination and anti-reflect properties. That is because the density of nuclear tracks is much higher than that in the Fig. 1b. Actually the tracks are even largely linked and overlapped with each other. The Fig.1b displays little anti-metal contamination and anti-reflect properties.

![](images/bb8bf649a8b72480a6e70632a2e4edeef4c157396bab3819493da9735c6b75da.jpg)

![](images/294c2c0a6eb0b53f803848a02fbfa46eb88cfe07926ba795321552c47cacd234.jpg)  
Fig.1 SEM of polyester

Fig. 2 is a SEM graph for mica. Compared to Fig. 1, the etched nuclear tracks in mica were rhombus (the rhombus was not flat), unlike the conical or cylindrical tracks in polyester. Mica is made of many layers. The etching solution might stay between two layers. That may lead to stair-like surface. The ability of anti-metal contamination was decreased greatly. And anti-reflectivity ability is complicated because of desquamation. So mica isn't suitable for studying anti-metal contamination and anti-reflect properties through surface modification by nuclear tracks.

![](images/6f452454a1a47afe2d333fa7f6da8716da5d87f4b0630848a879650f5f9ed99f.jpg)  
Fig. 2 The SEM of mica

# 中文摘要

实验中所选材料为绝缘材料，成分为有机高分子聚合物聚酯、聚碳酸酯和无机材料云母，应用固体核径迹的原理来研究材料表面的一些绝缘和光学性质。样品经过单能重离子辐照，溶液腐蚀蚀刻后的电子扫描电镜图片表明，径迹孔的孔径和孔深决定了样品表面的微结构，从而使样品具有不同的抗金属污染和抗反射性质。

# 14 Nuclear Recoiled Quenching Factor Measurement for a HPGe Detector Using a Monoenergetic Neutron Beam

Ruan Xichao $^{1}$ , Xin Biao $^{1}$ , Zhou Zuying $^{1}$ , Jiang Jing $^{1}$ , Huang Hanxiong $^{1}$ , Bao Jie $^{1}$ , Chen Lin $^{1}$ , Tang Hongqing $^{1}$ , Zhong Qiping $^{1}$ , Qi Bujia $^{1}$ , H.T. Wong $^{2}$ , G.Q. Zong $^{2}$ , Yue Qian $^{3}$ , Li Jing $^{4}$

(1. China Institute of Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-46, Beijing 102413, China;

2. Institute of Physics, Academia Sinica, Taipei, Taiwan, China;

3. Department of Engineering Physics, Tsinghua University, Beijing 100084, China;

4. Institute of High Energy Physics, Chinese Academy of Sciences, Beijing 100049, China)

Since $1985^{[1]}$ , various experimental techniques have been proposed to detect the nuclear recoils that would be produced by the scattering of WIMP dark matter particles from our galactic halo (for a recent review of experimental searches, see e.g. Ref. [2]). The goal is to be sensitive to recoil energies down to $10\mathrm{keV}^{[3]}$ , at event rates well below one per kilogram of detector material per day.[4]. For this, the detector response to low energy nuclear recoils must be well known. Depending on the detection process (ionization, scintillation or heat), this response may differ significantly from that inferred from calibration

with electron or $\gamma$ -ray sources. A quantity of interest is thus the quenching factor, defined as the ratio of the signal amplitudes induced by a nuclear recoil and an electron of the same energy. This factor depends mainly on the detector material, the energy of the recoil and the detection process, although temperature and alignment effects may play a role (see e.g. Ref. [5]).

# 1 Experimental Procedure

# 1.1 Principle and Experimental Setup

The basic method for the measurement of the quenching factor of a detector is the following. First, the detector response to electron recoils is obtained using calibrated $\gamma$ -ray source. This yields the calibration of the signal amplitude in terms of an electron-equivalent energy, $E_{\mathrm{ee}}$ . In a second step, the detector is exposed to a source producing nuclear recoils with a known kinetic energy $E_{\mathrm{R}}$ . The quenching factor $Q$ is then

$$
Q = E _ {\mathrm {e e}} / E _ {\mathrm {R}}
$$

The main difficulty for a precise and efficient measurement is the production of recoils with known $E_{\mathbb{R}}$ values. In the present method, nuclear recoils are produced by neutron scattering.

The experimental setup is shown in Fig.1. The pulsed neutron beam was produced by the ${}^{1}\mathrm{H}(^{7}\mathrm{Li},\mathrm{n})$ reaction using a hydrogen gas target and pulsed ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ beam. An HPGe detector was placed at the center of the neutron beam as an active scattering target and BC501 neutron detectors were placed at different angles to detect scattered neutrons from germanium. The neutron detectors were placed outside of the cone of neutron beam to avoid background from the source. The distances between the neutron source and HPGe detector and between HPGe detector and neutron detectors are $1\mathrm{m}$ . The shadow bar was used to reduce the background from the target (some background neutrons can be produced by ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ beam interact with structural materials). The coincidence between recoiled Ge signals from HPGe detector and elastic scattered neutrons by germanium from neutron detectors can give the pulse height of a recoiled Ge with a definite energy. Combination with the $\gamma$ calibration of the HPGe detector, we can determine the quenching factor as a function of recoiled energy.

![](images/be8b361fcb540f0473c9c7ed92d12b153f036026d741cde6747f3c3f4d673125.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Schematic view of the experimental setup

# 1.2 Neutron Beam and Detectors

The inverse kinematics reaction, ${}^{1}\mathrm{H}({}^{7}\mathrm{Li},\mathrm{n})$ reaction[6], was used to generate neutrons. The advantage for this neutron source is that it is shelf collimated. This means that the neutron emission open angle is

small and all generated neutrons emit in the forward angle (see Fig.1). This will enhance the neutron flux on the target and also reduce the background. It is not necessary to use heavy shielding to get collimated neutron beam.

A hydrogen gas target was used to produce neutrons. The gas volume is $10\mathrm{mm}$ in diameter and 5 mm in length and filled with 1 atm. hydrogen. The entrance foil is about $3\mu \mathrm{m}$ thickness molybdenum and the beam stop is $0.3\mathrm{mm}$ thickness gold. Pulsed ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ beam was accelerated to generate pulsed neutrons, thus the scattered neutrons by the germanium can be detected by TOF technique. In this condition, the neutron source is quite clean, with very small background.

A HPGe detector with effective 3cc volume was placed at 0 degree and $1\mathrm{m}$ distant from the neutron source. The noise of the HPGe detector is very low, below $2\mathrm{keV}$ of $E_{\mathrm{ee}}$ , and the resolution is $1.41\%$ for $59.54\mathrm{keV}$ ( $^{241}\mathrm{Am}$ ) and $16.32\%$ for $5.898\mathrm{keV}$ ( $^{55}\mathrm{Fe}$ ), respectively.

Three BC501 neutron detectors with $\phi 5\times 2$ inch were used to detect scattered neutrons by TOF technique. The detection thresholds were set low enough to detect the scattered neutrons from low energy group of the neutron source ( $< 0.5\mathrm{MeV}$ ) and also the pulse-shape-discrimination (PSD) was used to suppress $\gamma$ -background.

# 1.3 Electronics and DAQ System

For each neutron detector, three signals-pulse height(PH), Pulse shape(PSD) and TOF were recorded by list mode(event by event). For HPGe detector, the nuclear recoils(ER) ware recorded. An event was defined as the coincidence between HPGe detector and one of the neutron detectors. Therefore, there are four parameters(ER, PH, PSD and TOF) for every event. They were coupled into CAMAC ADC for AD conversion and recorded by Kmax[7] DAQ system with event by event mode.

# 1.4 Measurement

First, the HPGe detector was calibrated with $^{55}\mathrm{Fe}$ and $^{241}\mathrm{Am}$ sources, then two measurements were done for quenching factor measurement. The parameters for the two measurements are listed in table 1.

# 2 Data Analysis and Result

First, the $\gamma$ calibration for the HPGe detector was analyzed. From these calibrations, a linear function was fitted to the $E_{\mathrm{ee}}$ as a function of channels.

Table 1 Parameters for two measurements   

<table><tr><td>No.</td><td>E(7Li)/MeV</td><td>En/MeV</td><td>Detection angles/°</td><td>Recoiled energy for the 2En/keV</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Measurement No.1</td><td rowspan="3">17.85</td><td>3.24(high)</td><td>90</td><td>89, 17</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">0.62(low)</td><td>120</td><td>132, 25</td></tr><tr><td>150</td><td>163, 31</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="3">Measurement No.2</td><td rowspan="3">16.5</td><td>2.35(high)</td><td>50</td><td>23, 8</td></tr><tr><td rowspan="2">0.86(low)</td><td>90</td><td>64, 23</td></tr><tr><td>120</td><td>96, 35</td></tr></table>

Then, for each event, the coincidence of HPGe detector and neutron detector and PSD of the neutron detector were used to gate the TOF spectrum. Thus we can get the neutron TOF spectrum scatted from

germanium. From the TOF spectrum, the peak of the elastic scattering from the high and low energy group of neutrons can be seen. Then, the elastic scattering events were used to gate the $E_{\mathrm{R}}$ of HPGe detector.

Now, we can get the recoiled germanium peak positions (in channels or $E_{\mathrm{ee}}$ ) as a function of recoil energy and the quenching factor can be easily determined. Fig.2 shows the preliminary result for this experiment.

![](images/974427a1b83759a8ef3eeb2ab6d852337055c62d2aaac2cd511d64f624a5be1a.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Preliminary result of the quenching factor   
The uncertainties were given by sigma of the Gaussian fit

# 中文摘要

探测暗物质的一种常用的方法是测量暗物质粒子引起的核反冲，因此，所用的探测器在使用前必须精确知道其猝灭因子。本工作利用单能的低能中子引起核反冲的方法测量了HPGe探测器的猝灭因子。中子源采用 ${}^{1}\mathrm{H}(^{7}\mathrm{Li},\mathrm{n})$ 反应中子源，入射 ${}^{7}\mathrm{Li}$ 的有效能量为 $13.5\mathrm{MeV}$ ，在 $0^{\circ}$ 产生了2.35和 $0.85\mathrm{MeV}$ 两组单能中子，用中子探测器探测不同角度的散射中子，与相应的反冲Ge信号符合，便可得到不同反冲核能量的脉冲幅度及相应的猝灭因子。实验测量了一系列能点的猝灭因子，最低反冲核能量达到了 $8\mathrm{keV}$ 。并对测量结果与其他测量结果进行了比较。

# AND DETECTOR

![](images/94d0af0fad385321484e0d6bd425819cdb46cf001f74c8f34e94164ed9544605.jpg)

# 1 Preparative Technologies for Three-Layer-Sandwich Targets Used by Measuring $g$ -Factor of Rotational Levels

Fan Qiwen, Xu Guoji

(China Institute Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-62, Beijing 102413, China)

The $g$ -factor of the positive parity rotation band states needed to be measured by a TMF-IMPAD (transient-magnetic-field ion implantation perturbed angular distribution) method in the HI-13 tandem accelerator nuclear physics national laboratory at China Institute of Atomic Energy. Therefore, the three-layer-sandwich targets of ${}^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn - Fe - Au}$ needed to be prepared. The middle layers were thin ferromagnetic Fe foils of $1.15 - 1.52\mathrm{mg / cm}^2$ , recoil stopper layers were thick crystallized and defect-free Au layers of $3 - 4\mathrm{mg / cm}^2$ . The thicknesses of the ${}^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ target layers were $200 - 400\mu \mathrm{g / cm}^2$ . The uniformity should be better than $92\%$ .

# 1 Preparation of Thin Ferromagnetic Fe Middle Layers by Rolling-Annealing

Fe foils of $1 - 2\mathrm{mg} / \mathrm{cm}^2$ thickness with good magnetic properties have been prepared by rolling-annealing. The process was following: the original thick iron foils were put in the stainless steel sandwich, and then were rolled with the same direction, then Fe foils were annealed. The Fe annealing was carried out at about $950\mathrm{K}$ , and lasted for about $1\mathrm{h}$ in a vacuum better than $10^{-4}\mathrm{Pa}$ .

# 2 Preparation of Au Recoil Stopper Layers by Vacuum Evaporation-Condensation and Annealing

Au recoil stopper layers have been prepared by resistance heating. The crucibles were made from Mo materials. In order to clean the iron foils, the glowing discharge was carried out before deposition of Au targets.

In order to get crystallized and defect-free Au recoil stopper layers, it was very critical to anneal Au foils deposited by vacuum evaporation. The Au/Fe annealing was carried out at about $600\mathrm{K}$ by resistance heating of Ta between two electrodes, and lasted for about 1 h in a vacuum better than $10^{-4}\mathrm{Pa}$ .

The experiments have demonstrated that the temperature of Au/Fe annealing can't be too high, because the iron will be diffused into Au layers at about $800\mathrm{K}$ .

# 3 Preparation of Target Layers

As we all know, it is very difficult to fabricate homogenous isotopic Zn target layers. And we should take most economic methods to prepare them, because these isotopes are very expensive.

The schematic drawing of the rotating substrate setup is shown in Fig. 1.

Behrndt expresses the film thickness by the following equation.

$$
t (s) = \frac {m}{\pi \rho} \frac {h ^ {2} \left(h ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + s ^ {2}\right)}{\left[ \left(h ^ {2} + r ^ {2} + s ^ {2}\right) - 4 s ^ {2} r ^ {2} \right] ^ {3 / 2}} \tag {1}
$$

Where $m$ is the mass of the evaporated material, $\rho$ is the density of the evaporant, $h$ is the

source-to-substrate distance, and $r$ is the horizontal distance between source center and substrate axis, $s$ is the distance between substrate axis and an arbitrary point on the substrate.

![](images/8dee39e9ecbc1558393ff9db1059a37e7be17f3c2a776733aa45a3719e352281.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Schematic drawing of the rotating substrate set-up

According to this formula, film thickness distribution have been calculated and plotted by Matlab in Fig. 2 at $h = 1.0$ cm and different values of $r$ . In order to obtain the target thickness units commonly used in nuclear physics, the equation (1) need to be rearranged

$$
d (s) = 1 0 0 0 t (s) \frac {\rho}{m} \left(\mu \mathrm {g} \cdot \mathrm {c m} ^ {- 2} \cdot \mathrm {m g} ^ {- 1}\right) \tag {2}
$$

A differential collection efficiency for the individual target can be defined from the equation (2).

![](images/4d0f6edca2dc0d16bf983990e13284cde5ff1e04ab6de42becb40bbae0dea1d5.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Theoretical film thickness distributions for a source-to-substrate distance $h = 1.0$ cm

For a specific distance $h$ , an optimum distance $r$ can always be found which meets both the criteria of high collection efficiency and good target uniformity. This optimum depends on the spatial installation of the desired target area on the rotating substrate, which is represented by the range of the parameter $s$ .

In the practical process of evaporation-deposition, the following was very key and important.

1) We must take glow discharge to clean the Fe substrates, or it was very difficult to deposit the homogenous $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ target layers on the iron substrates.

2) As $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ materials have a low melt, a small crucible must be made, or homogeneity $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ target layers can't be deposited due to bake.   
3) It was necessary to use cleaning Al foils to cover the upper Fe foils so that the upper Fe foils were not polluted by $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ vapor.

$^{67,68,70}$ Zn target layers which meet both the criteria of high collection efficiency and good target uniformity have successfully been prepared by rotated substrate under the condition of $h = 1.0 \, \text{cm}$ , $r = 1.2 \, \text{cm}$ and $s = 0.5 - 1.4 \, \text{cm}$ . Their thicknesses are respectively $350 \, \mu \text{g/cm}^2$ , $368 \, \mu \text{g/cm}^2$ and $324 \, \mu \text{g/cm}^2$ , and respectively use 4.3, 3.5 and $4.6 \, \text{mg}$ isotopic materials.

# 中文摘要

本文研究了测量转动能级的 $g$ 因子用靶 $(^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn - Fe - Au})$ 的制备技术。这是一种特殊要求的三层夹心靶，中间层是要求有铁磁性的Fe膜，后面的阻止层是要求有立方晶格结构的Au膜，前面的反应层包括同位素 $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ 。本文采用滚轧退火制备有铁磁性的Fe膜，用真空蒸发-退火制备有立方晶格结构的Au膜，用转动衬底法制备 $^{67,68,70}\mathrm{Zn}$ 靶。

# 2 Preparation of Self-Supporting Targets With Thickness of Less Than $100\mu \mathrm{g} / \mathrm{cm}^2$ by Focused Heavy-Ion Beam Sputtering

Fan Qiwen, Xu Guoji

(China Institute Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-62, Beijing 102413, China)

Low energy nuclear reaction studies require thin, preferably self-supporting targets in order to get optimum resolution. Standard techniques for producing them include resistance heating, electron-beam bombardment and focused heavy-ion beam sputtering. High vacuum sputter deposition is a very good method of preparing high melting point targets and isotopic expensive metal targets. Focused heavy-ion beam sputtering was used to produce self-supporting, thin targets justifiable. Copper foils with thickness of $10 - 20\mu \mathrm{m}$ worked as substrates, and target frames' square is $0.9~\mathrm{cm}^2$ . And when etchant, $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ or $\mathrm{CCl}_3\mathrm{CO}_2\mathrm{NH}_4$ was used, we found it resulted in strong, little stress foils.

# 1 Principle and Equipment

The basic principle of focused heavy-ion beam sputtering: $\mathrm{Ar^{+}}$ beam produced by ion source is brought into high vacuum chamber by extraction electrode, and then is accelerated to $10\mathrm{keV}$ . We can obtain $\mathrm{Ar^{+}}$ beam coverage of $\phi 1 - 2\mathrm{mm}$ in the cathode of graphite crucible by adjusting the focusing function of single-lens and the discharge-status of ion source. High beam intensity bombard the materials, a quantity of the atoms of the material are sputtered out and deposited onto the substrate to form a homogenous thin foils. The schematic setup for Ar-beam-induced sputtering deposition is shown in Fig. 1.

Our equipment of focused heavy-ion beam sputtering is mainly made of penning ion source, accelerating and focusing lens, sputtered and rotated unit, the vacuum system.

# 2 Substrate and Aqua Erosion

Cu foils worked as substrates, and $50\%$ $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ or $\mathrm{CCl}_3\mathrm{CO}_2\mathrm{NH}_4$ (9 g $\mathrm{CCl}_3\mathrm{CO}_2\mathrm{H} + 45\mathrm{mL}$ $\mathrm{NH}_4\mathrm{OH} + 45\mathrm{mL}$ $\mathrm{H}_2\mathrm{O}$ ) used as aqua erosion.

![](images/c0491a80fe6ac3a28dc19fcfb22ced57c79fe5ce08b223aa6e0a94bafe2ebda6.jpg)  
Fig. 1 Schematic setup for $\mathbf{Ar}^{+}$ -beam-induced sputtering deposition

# 3 Process and Results

Cu foils prepared by rolling are installed on target frame, and embedded sputtering chamber. After sputtering chamber is evacuated into high vacuum by mechanical pump and oil diffusion pump, we control the parameters of sputtering (table 1), the quantity of target materials are deposited onto substrate to form target foils. After the system was cooled, putting substrates deposited foils out, using $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ or $\mathrm{CCl}_3\mathrm{CO}_2\mathrm{NH}_4$ to erode the substrates, the foils are levitated on deionized water and salvaged on our standard target frame of $0.9~\mathrm{cm}^2$ . The results are shown in table 2.

Table 1 Parameters of sputtering

# 中文摘要

本文讲述了用聚焦重离子溅射法来制备厚度 $< 100\mu \mathrm{g} / \mathrm{cm}^2$ 的自支撑薄靶的技术，采用Cu作为基衬，用 $\mathrm{HNO}_3$ 或 $\mathrm{CCl}_3\mathrm{CO}_2\mathrm{NH}_4$ 作为腐蚀基衬的溶液，并用天平称重法测量其厚度。

# 3 HI-13 Tandem Accelerator in 2005

Kan Chaoxin, Hu Yueming, Bao Yiwen, Fan Hongsheng, Yang Bingfan (China Institute Atomic Energy, P. O. Box 275-62, Beijing 102413, China)

HI-13 tandem accelerator was running well in 2005, the machine has been operated for $4700\mathrm{h}$ , and provided beam time $4000\mathrm{h}$ for more than 30 experiment users, the terminal voltage range is from $2\mathrm{MV}$ to $13\mathrm{MV}$ .

# 1 Operation

In 2005 HI-13 tandem accelerator provided 18 kinds of ions and $4000\mathrm{h}$ beam time.

The distribution of beam time versus ion and month is shown in Fig.1 and Fig.2.

![](images/8f2531d33375bedb36d2ad9dd4ff8079e0ff1a72a1d08a28bb8c08a3cf77f1b6.jpg)  
Fig.1 Distribution of beam time versus ion

![](images/4d29ede8618968190a9b5f44adffad59f4478e8618d42e45bd9450e74cb725e4.jpg)  
Fig. 2 Distribution of time versus month

# 2 Maintenance and Development

1) The trouble is high frequency in this year, the tank was opened 7 times to change the laddertron and deal with some problems.   
2) Finished some replacement of the accelerator subassembly.   
3) The project for injector upgrading: Finished the machine design, machining, order of some device and the design of control system, provided the condition to install.   
4) Finished the installation and modulation of the speed selector for $\mathbf{R}50^{\circ}$ beam line.   
5) The project for the accelerator radiation protection and safety technology development has been started, the tandem accelerator will be running safely and stability in a long time.   
6) Provided 400 nuclear targets and 400 pieces of strip films to users, exploder some new technics to make targets.

In 2006, the injector upgrade project will be finished; the new injector will be running. The development of beam line $\mathbf{R}20^{\circ}$ and $\mathbf{R}70^{\circ}$ will be carried through. Make good conditions for the machine and keep it running in high quality and efficiency.
